**(a) THE SENTENCE THIS RE-DERIVES, QUOTED EXACTLY as the `F-RA-13` clause's item `(4)` carries it: *"no user code the caller supplied as the refused value's conversion path runs on a refusal path, except (i) where that path lies on a prototype judged language-standard by shape at the two conditional arms AND IS AN OVERRIDDEN `toString` or `valueOf`; (ii) at the three states item `(1)` declares refusable, until their code lands; and (iii) at a `Proxy` standing as a prototype link, until the two guards of `§2`'s clause of this date on `F-RA-12` land."*** **IT IS NOT EXACTLY TRUE IN TWO WAYS: its exception `(i)` OMITS THE OVERRIDDEN `join` that the same clause's item `(2)` names as part of the SAME residual (*"and, at an array's `toString`, an overridden `join`, which `Array.prototype.toString` calls"*), and its exceptions `(ii)` and `(iii)` read *"until their code lands"* although THOSE BYTES HAVE LANDED.**
**(b) THE SENTENCE FROM THIS DATE, AND IT IS THE ONE A DONE ROW QUOTES: *no user code the caller supplied as the refused value's conversion path runs on a refusal path, except where that path lies on a prototype judged language-standard by shape at the two conditional arms AND IS AN OVERRIDDEN `toString`, `valueOf`, or — at an array's own `toString` — AN OVERRIDDEN `join`.***
**(c) THE TWO DISCHARGED EXCEPTIONS, RECORDED SO THE QUOTED SENTENCE CARRIES NO STALE CONDITION: exception `(ii)` — the three states of item `(1)` — is DISCHARGED: item `(1a)`'s `Symbol.toPrimitive` descriptor read (`:1191`, taken before the exemption stops the walk), item `(1b)`'s `Symbol.toStringTag` descriptor read (`:1224`–`:1238`) and item `(1c)`'s element reads (`:1252`–`:1270`) are in the tree, their cells `JC-TC-52`, `JC-TC-53` and `JC-TC-54` stand in `bindings/js/test/json-crossing.test.mjs` (`:894` · `:1011` · `:1115`), and item `(1)`'s rule is TRUE at them; exception `(iii)` — a `Proxy` standing as a prototype link — is DISCHARGED: `isStandardObjectPrototype`'s guard at `:860` and `arrayClass`'s at `:946` are in the tree, its cell `JC-TC-51` stands at `:747`, and no trap runs at either decision.**
**(d) WHAT IS STILL EXCEPTED AND WHY IT IS STILL THE WHOLE OF THE RESIDUAL: the OVERRIDE family alone — `toString`, `valueOf`, and the array's `join` — for the reason item `(2)` states and only that reason: the carrying test asks whether the NAME is own and never whether the member's VALUE is the intrinsic, and no read this contract allows distinguishes an intrinsic from an override, so closing it needs a pristine identity and `C-11` must be re-opened first.** **Item `(9)`'s sub-item `(e)` — re-derived to *"the three owed states of item `(1)`"* — is therefore DISCHARGED with them, and the sentence's exception list is ONE family and NOTHING ELSE.**
**(e) NO CODE IS OWED BY THIS FINDING, AND NOTHING MOVES WITH IT: no class, no status, no position, no cell, no arm and no run figure; the residual's size, its owner and its revisit triggers stand.** **AND A DONE ROW MAY NOW QUOTE THE SENTENCE AT ITEM `(b)` WITHOUT A DATE QUALIFIER FOR `(ii)` OR `(iii)`, WHILE IT MAY NOT CLAIM THE RESIDUAL CLOSED.**

**(6) `F-RA-23` — ITEM `(1a)`'S LETTER GAINS THE HOP ITS OWN REMEDY TAKES: THE `@@toPrimitive` READ CONTINUES ABOVE A SHAPE-STANDARD LINK.**
**(a) THE LETTER'S SHORTFALL: item `(1a)` says the read is *"ONE OWN-DESCRIPTOR READ OF `Symbol.toPrimitive` PER LINK, TAKEN BEFORE THE EXEMPTION STOPS THE WALK"*, and a reader can take *"before the exemption stops the walk"* to mean that the read ENDS where the `toString`/`valueOf` search ends. IT DOES NOT: the read is taken at EVERY link the chain reaches, INCLUDING EVERY LINK ABOVE THE FIRST SHAPE-STANDARD ONE, because ToPrimitive's `@@toPrimitive` lookup is a `[[Get]]` that traverses the WHOLE chain.**
**(b) THE CODE, AT ITS LINES: `carriesConversionMember` (`:1182`) takes the `@@toPrimitive` own-descriptor read at `:1191` BEFORE the exemption flag is consulted, and the flag (`aboveTheExemption`, `:1187` · `:1196`) governs ONLY the `toString`/`valueOf` branch at `:1197` — so `:1191` runs at `:1196`'s link and at every link after it, and the loop ends at `:1201`'s `null` and nowhere earlier.** **The file's own comment at that site states it: *"the `toString`/`valueOf` search ends here, the `@@toPrimitive` read above does not"* (`:1194`–`:1195`).**
**(c) THE STATE THE HOP IS FOR, AND ITS WITNESS IN THE PROPERTY LAYER: a `Symbol.toPrimitive` ADDED to `Object.prototype` is reached at a refused FUNCTION at row `3`, whose chain is the function → `Function.prototype` (a shape-standard link, where the exemption stops) → `Object.prototype` (where the added member stands). Item `(1a)`'s read answers "carries" at that ABOVE link, which is what makes the row-`3` half of `JC-TC-52` green; `NG-JS-Q12`'s SECOND VARIANT (`bindings/js/test/negative-generators.test.mjs:5495`) is the drive that asserts it — `{ arm: 'row3', carrier: 'Object.prototype' }` — and no drive at `Function.prototype` alone would reach the state.**
**(d) THE LETTER FROM THIS DATE, AND NOTHING ELSE OF ITEM `(1a)` MOVES: *"ONE OWN-DESCRIPTOR READ OF `Symbol.toPrimitive` PER LINK, TAKEN BEFORE THE EXEMPTION STOPS THE WALK AND CONTINUING ABOVE EVERY SHAPE-STANDARD LINK THE CHAIN STILL REACHES — because `[[Get]]` for `@@toPrimitive` traverses the whole chain, so a member added ABOVE the language's own prototype is reached by the rendering exactly as one added below it."***
**(e) NO CODE IS OWED BY THIS FINDING: the build takes the hop (item `(b)`), `NG-JS-Q12`'s second variant asserts it, and what was missing was the letter's own sentence. No class, no status, no cell, no arm and no admission moves.**

`(b)`'s operative half: *"from this date NO USER CODE RUNS AT A CARRIER POSITION'S SUBJECT RENDERING"*. **IT IS FALSE AS A UNIVERSAL, AND THE STATE THAT FALSIFIES IT IS ONE OF THAT CLAUSE'S OWN DECLARED SLOTS: `(d)(i)`'s descriptor IS THE VALUE'S OWN RENDERING** — that item's own words, *"THEIR DESCRIPTOR IS `docs/specs/storeroom-js/surface.md §2.5` item 5 (a)'s own value rendering — the `targetSpelling`.** **THE MEASURED STATE, TAKEN BY THE RED-CELL RUN AT A `(d)(i)` SLOT: an address carrying its own `toString` RUNS IT — the caller's marker `'OPMARK'` stands as that slot's subject and the counter reads `1`** — so the rendering is not merely reachable there, it is that slot's declared answer. **THE NARROWED LAW, IN ITS OPERATIVE WORDS: *NO USER CODE THE CALLER SUPPLIED AS THE VALUE'S CONVERSION PATH RUNS AT A CARRIER POSITION WHOSE DECLARED DESCRIPTOR IS A CLASS OR A LITERAL.*** **AND THE COMPLEMENT, DECLARED SO IT IS NOT FILED AS A LEAK: *A CARRIER POSITION WHOSE DECLARED **(1) `F5-1` — THE GUARD'S COST IS BOUNDED PER CALL: THE PATH DECIDES THE TRIP, A VISITED SET CARRIES THE COST, AND A POSITION BUDGET ANSWERS THE CLASS SPELLING ON EXHAUSTION.**

**(1a) THE COST THE SHIPPED GUARD PAYS, READ AT THE BYTES AND WITHOUT A RUN: `arrayElementsReachCallerCode` (`bindings/js/index.mjs:1263`) decides its trip with `if (path.includes(value)) return true;` (`:1268`) and extends the path with `const next = [...path, value];` (`:1269`), recursing through `renderGuardTrips(descriptor.value, next)` (`:1275`). THERE IS NO VISITED SET, SO A SHARED SUB-VALUE IS WALKED ONCE PER POSITION IT OCCUPIES: for `let v = [1]; for (let i = 0; i < 30; i += 1) v = [v, v];` — a `160`-element input — the traversal is `T(d) = 2·T(d−1)`, i.e. `~2^30` recursive visits, and at depth `40` `~2^40`.** **NEITHER CEILING OF `§4` COUNTS IT, and the reason is structural rather than an oversight: `encodeValue` refuses such a value at row `8`'s DEPTH arm ON ENTRY (`:1600`–`:1602`), so the refused SUBTREE IS NEVER WALKED and neither `NODE_CEILING` (`:1489`) nor `ENCODER_CEILING` (`:1488`) bounds the guard's traversal of it.** **AND THE SAME LIMB SCANS THE WHOLE `length` — `const length = value.length;` (`:1270`), `for (let i = 0; i < length; i += 1)` (`:1271`), `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, i)` (`:1272`) — so `arrayChain(new Array(4294967295), 127)` costs `~4.29×10^9` descriptor reads at that arm, while the WALK's own hole scan would refuse the same sparse array AT ITS FIRST HOLE (`:1661`, `Object.hasOwn(value, i)`, `i = 0`) in one read. THE TWO LIMBS ARE ONE LAW, and the second is recorded at item `(2)`.**

**(1b) THE LAW'S THREE LIMBS, EACH STATED SO A PROPERTY IS WRITTEN FROM IT AND NOT FROM A GUESS:**
**(i) THE TRIP IS THE ANCESTOR/PATH TEST, AND THE `F-RA-20` RULING IS UNMOVED: a value ON the current path trips (a cycle); a value OFF it does not (an alias).** The membership test is taken FIRST, before any cost memo, because an in-progress value is by construction on the path.
**(ii) THE COST IS CARRIED BY A VISITED SET — a per-call memo keyed on the value — CONSULTED ONLY AFTER THE PATH TEST, whose answer for a repeat is `false` (the answer its first occurrence gave) and whose recorded figure is the repeat's own position count.** The honest form, printed so the implementer's byte is not a guess: `if (path.includes(value)) return true; if (visited.has(value)) return <the recorded answer and count>;` — **SO THE TRAVERSAL IS LINEAR IN THE DISTINCT VALUES AND THE TRIP ANSWER IS UNCHANGED.** **ITS SOUNDNESS IS TWO FACTS AND NOT AN APPEAL TO INTENT: a `true` is never withheld, because a `true` returns through every enclosing frame (`:1275`), so a frame that is still scanning has produced no `true` yet; and an in-progress value is ON the path, so limb `(i)` answers it before limb `(ii)` is consulted.** **`F-RA-20`'s items `(b)`, `(c)`, `(e)` and `(f)` STAND: `JC-TC-56`'s alias renders `'1,1'`, its cycle answers row `8`'s class spelling, and the memo is a COST device and never a trip decision — so `JC-TC-56`'s limit *"no cell may assert a subject derived from a visited-set reading"* is READ FROM THIS DATE as *"no cell may assert a subject derived from a visited set taken as a TRIP DECISION"* (`test-cases.md`'s clause of this date, item `(5)`).**
**(iii) A PER-CALL POSITION BUDGET, AND THE ANSWER WHEN IT IS SPENT: THE GUARD MAY STAND FOR NO MORE THAN `1,000,000` POSITIONS PER CALL OF `renderGuardTrips` (`:1289`) — `NODE_CEILING`'s own figure, this contract's own counting convention (`§8` row `8`: one node per POSITION the walk visits, a shared sub-value counted once per position) — AND ON EXHAUSTION THE GUARD ANSWERS "CALLER CODE IS REACHABLE", the WITH branch, so the arm answers its CLASS SPELLING and renders nothing.** **THE ONE FIGURE BOUNDS TWO THINGS: the positions the traversal STANDS FOR (so an aliased DAG is counted per position and the budget cannot be evaded by the memo) AND the descriptor reads the element limb performs (so a single array's `length` is never a spent allowance).** **A FRESH BUDGET PER CALL — the entry is `renderGuardTrips`'s default (`:1289`, `path = []`), which the carrier slot reaches through the guard call (`:1354`) and the value arm through `valueTargetSpelling` (`:1316`) — NEVER PER ELEMENT AND NEVER MODULE-LEVEL, so no census is cached across calls and `C-11`'s no-frozen-prototype-fact rule is untouched.**

**(1c) WHY THE BUDGET IS ON POSITIONS AND NOT ON DISTINCT DESCRIPTOR READS — THE REASON BOTH LIMBS ARE NEEDED, STATED SO NO PASS LANDS A LINEAR GUARD AND CLAIMS THE COST CLOSED:** a `false` from this limb AUTHORIZES A RENDERING, and `String(value)` on an aliased DAG DUPLICATES THE SHARED SUBTREE (`Array.prototype.toString` → `join` converts each of the two elements), so the rendering's own size is `2^d` exactly where the path test's visit count was `2^d`. **A DISTINCT-READ COUNT ON `let v = [1]; for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) v = [v, v];` IS `~80` AND WOULD NEVER TRIP, while the rendering that answer approves is `~2^40` characters — SO A MEMO ALONE MOVES THE EXPONENT FROM THE GUARD TO THE RENDERING AND CLOSES NOTHING. The position budget is the limb that closes it, and it closes it at the figure the crossing already refuses: a refused value whose position count exceeds `NODE_CEILING` is a value the walk's own node ceiling would have refused had it walked it (`§4`'s node clause, `§8` row `8`).**

**(1d) THE FIGURE AND ITS GROUND, NAMED SO IT IS NOT A SECOND CEILING: `1,000,000` POSITIONS IS `NODE_CEILING` (`:1489`), and NO THIRD CEILING IS MINTED.** The grounds are three: it is the crossing's own declared bound on the work one value may cost; it is the figure at which the walk itself refuses the same shape, so the guard's answer and the walk's are one answer at one boundary; and the counting convention it uses is `§8` row `8`'s (positions, an alias counted per position), so the guard's arithmetic and the walk's arithmetic are ONE arithmetic. **AND THE DECLARED CONSEQUENCE, STATED SO IT IS NOT FILED AS A DEFECT: a refused value at row `3` or at row `8`'s depth arm whose element positions exceed `1,000,000` answers its CLASS SPELLING from this date where an unbounded guard would have rendered it — an over-refusal of a value the crossing would not have carried, in the direction `C-11`'s fail-closed answer permits, and no cell may assert such a state as an admission.**

**(1e) THE OBSERVABLE THE CELL PINS, NAMED EXACTLY: `arrayChain(ladder, 127)` with `let v = [1]; for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) v = [v, v];` — a `~2^40`-visit state for the shipped guard — ANSWERS ROW `8`'S CLASS SPELLING `a value over the walk ceiling` at the depth arm's own `127`-segment position, AND THE WHOLE REFUSAL COMPLETES WITHIN A WALL-CLOCK CEILING OF `5` SECONDS.** **The ceiling's ground: the declared work is at most `1,000,001` positions with one descriptor read apiece — on the order of tens of milliseconds on any machine that runs this suite — so `5` seconds carries some two orders of magnitude of headroom and still fails the unbounded form, whose drive is `~2^40` visits, by many orders.** **THE SECOND STATE, from item `(1a)`'s other limb: `arrayChain(new Array(4294967295), 127)` answers the same class spelling at the same position inside the same ceiling, its `4.29×10^9` reads never taken.** **AND THE CONTROLS, WHICH MUST NOT MOVE: `'1,1'` for `JC-TC-56`'s aliased `[e, e]` (`4` positions), `'1,2'` for a primitive `[1, 2]` at the depth arm, `'[object Object]'` for `{}`, `'1,,3'` for a sparse `[1, , 3]` (its hole `continue`d exactly as `join` continues it, `:1273`), and a function's own source text at row `3`.**

**(1f) A HOLE IS NOT A TRIP AND MUST NOT BE MADE ONE: the element limb `continue`s where `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, i)` is `undefined` (`:1272`–`:1273`) because `join` converts nothing at a hole, and `'1,,3'`'s rendering is declared — so an answer of "caller code" at a hole would be an over-refusal this law does not declare. What bounds the sparse limb is the budget and NOTHING ELSE.** **AND A SPARSE ARRAY IS REFUSED BY THE WALK'S OWN HOLE SCAN AT ITS FIRST HOLE (`:1661`) wherever the walk reaches it, so the guard must not OUT-WALK the walk: it may not spend a `length` the walk would never have walked.**

**(1g) THE FORM IS THE IMPLEMENTER'S WHERE THE THREE LIMBS HOLD: the site is `arrayElementsReachCallerCode` (`:1263`) and the default at `renderGuardTrips` (`:1289`); the descriptor reads (`:1272`–`:1274`), the accessor answer (`:1274`), the `Proxy` answer (`:1266`), the non-array answer (`:1267`) and the guarded `catch` (`:1278`–`:1280`) STAND — and that `catch`'s existing answer, the class spelling for a traversal that throws (a stack exhaustion included), is a FOURTH bound already in the file and is unmoved.**

**(1h) A CODE CHANGE IS OWED, RED-FIRST: the cell is `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-57`, which carries both states and the controls of item `(1e)`; the red comes FIRST and the bytes come second (`implementation-plan.md §2` step `4`'s clause of this date, arm `(viii)`).** **UNTIL THEY LAND: no cell may assert a subject at either state, no cell may assert the guard's cost as the law, and THE UNIT MAY NOT CLAIM THAT ITS RENDER GUARD IS BOUNDED.**

**(2) `F5-2` — THE ELEMENT LIMB'S `length` SCAN IS ITEM `(1)`'S SAME BUDGET, DECLARED HERE SO THE TWO FINDINGS ARE NOT LANDED AS TWO BYTES.**
**(a) THE STATE, AT ITS FIGURE: `arrayChain(new Array(4294967295), 127)` performs `~4.29×10^9` descriptor reads at row `8`'s depth arm before that arm can answer, because the limb scans the WHOLE `length` (`:1270`–`:1272`) while the walk's own hole scan refuses a sparse array at its FIRST hole, `i = 0`, one read (`:1661`).**
**(b) THE LAW: the limb is subject to item `(1b)(iii)`'s SAME per-call budget — each index it reads counts as one position against the one figure — and ON EXHAUSTION IT ANSWERS THE CLASS SPELLING. The state, the figure, the conservative answer and the observable are items `(1a)`, `(1d)` and `(1e)`, and the walk's own precedence (the hole scan `:1661` before the shape helper `:1663` and before the element loop `:1693`) means a sparse array never reaches the guard from a walked position at all.**
**(c) NO ADDITIONAL CODE IS OWED BEYOND ARM `(viii)` AND NO SECOND CELL: `JC-TC-57`'s second state is this state, and the same byte answers both.**

**(3) `F5-3` — A `Proxy`, CALLABLE OR NOT, IS NEVER COERCED AT A CARRIER SLOT: THE `(d)(i)` DESCRIPTOR'S `Proxy` LIMB IS DECLARED TO READ "OR CALLABLE", AND THE ONE-TEST CHANGE IS OWED RED-FIRST.**

**(3a) THE BYTES, WHICH ARE THE DEFECT: `targetSpelling` (`:1020`) guards a `Proxy` only when `typeof value === 'object'` — `if (typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && types.isProxy(value)) return classSpelling(value);` (`:1034`) — so a CALLABLE `Proxy` falls past the guard to `String(value)` (`:1035`), whose `ToPrimitive` reads `Symbol.toPrimitive` and then `toString` THROUGH THE `get` TRAP.** **THE SIBLING ARM ALREADY TESTS OBJECT *OR* FUNCTION: `valueTargetSpelling` (`:1316`) guards with `(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') && value !== null && types.isProxy(value)` (`:1327`) — so the two renderers disagree at exactly the class that reads `typeof 'function'`, which `§2`'s clause of this date on the PBT audit's `F7` finding declares: a callable `Proxy`'s CLASS is `a function` (row `3`) while its `<subject>` is `a Proxy`.** **AND THE STATE IS REACHABLE FROM THE PUBLIC SURFACE AT EVERY `(d)(i)` SLOT THAT RENDERS ITS ARGUMENT: `listen(callableProxy, [])` fails `inDomainHandle` (`:2232`) and answers through `targetSpelling(handle)` (`:3162`); a callable `Proxy` inside `listen`'s set answers through `targetSpelling(addresses[i])` (`:3186` · `:3194`); `removeListener(callableProxy)` answers through `targetSpelling(handle)` (`:3535`); `register(callableProxy, fn)` answers through `targetSpelling(handle)` (`:4140`); and `encodeOpId(callableProxy)` answers through `targetSpelling(opId)` (`:2210`).** **THE LISTENER SLOT AT `:4126` IS NOT ONE OF THEM and no cell may drive it as one: `register`'s `typeof listener !== 'function'` test (`:4123`) PASSES for a callable `Proxy`, so that slot never renders one.**

**(3b) THE LAW, IN ITS OPERATIVE WORDS: A `Proxy` — CALLABLE OR NOT, REVOKED INCLUDED — IS NEVER COERCED AT ANY CARRIER SLOT; its subject is its CLASS SPELLING `a Proxy`, read with `util.types.isProxy` alone, and no trap runs.** **The `(d)(i)` descriptor's phrase is re-derived here, because it is prose and no cell carries it: its *"a `Proxy`'s class spelling"* reads FROM THIS DATE as *"a `Proxy`'s class spelling, CALLABLE OR NOT"* — the `Proxy` class is ONE class, and the renderer's `typeof` split is not a fact about it. `classSpelling` (`:995`–`:1006`) puts the `Proxy` limb (`:1000`) ABOVE the function limb (`:1002`), so a callable `Proxy`'s subject is `a Proxy` at its row `3` refusal.**

**(3c) THE ONE-TEST CHANGE, NAMED SO THE IMPLEMENTER IS OWED NO DESIGN: `:1034`'s test becomes the sibling arm's own form — `if ((typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') && value !== null && types.isProxy(value)) return classSpelling(value);`** — and the `bigint` limb (`:1026`), `String(value)` (`:1035`), the `try` (`:1021`), the `catch` (`:1036`–`:1037`), every other `(d)(i)`/`(d)(ii)`/`(d)(iii)` descriptor and `valueTargetSpelling`'s own guard (`:1327`) STAND. No identity is compared and no prototype is frozen: `C-11` is untouched.

**(3d) A CODE CHANGE IS OWED, RED-FIRST: the cell is `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-58` — a callable `Proxy` whose `get` trap returns a marker for `Symbol.toPrimitive` and counts, driven at a `(d)(i)` slot, answering the subject `a Proxy` with the trap counter at `0` and the marker reaching no byte — the red FIRST and the byte SECOND (arm `(ix)`).** **UNTIL IT LANDS: no cell may assert a trap-running callable-`Proxy` state at a carrier slot as the law, no cell may assert a caller-supplied answer as a carrier's subject, and NO DONE ROW MAY CLAIM `:1027`'S OWN COMMENT — *"a `Proxy` is never coerced, by this renderer either"* — AS A UNIVERSAL.**

**(4) `F5-4` — THE POSITION SPELLING IS RULED A DIAGNOSTIC-INTEGRITY BREACH AND A HOST FIX, NOT A DECLARED CONSEQUENCE: IT MAY INVOKE NOTHING TAKEN FROM A MUTABLE PROTOTYPE.**

**(4a) THE BYTES: `valuePosition` (`:1080`) builds the segment list with `path.map(...)` (`:1082`–`:1083`) and joins it with `.join('/')` (`:1094`) — `Array.prototype.map` and `Array.prototype.join` taken from a MUTABLE PROTOTYPE, so a caller's override RUNS ON EVERY MEMBER-POSITION REFUSAL AND ITS RETURN VALUE BECOMES THE POSITION.** **THE STATE IS MEASURED AT BOTH LAYERS AND NEITHER MEASUREMENT IS MINE TO RE-RUN: `bindings/js/test/negative-generators.test.mjs`'s `NG-JS-Q17` (`:5964`) drives an overridden `Array.prototype.join` at row `8`'s depth arm and records the counter at `2` with the POSITION reading the marker (`:5957`); and `docs/specs/storeroom-json-crossing-live-report.md`'s `LIVE-F18` (`§12B`) records the same second run and names it *"a SECOND CALLER-CODE PATH THE ARCHITECT SHOULD RECORD WITH THE RESIDUAL"*, its end-state's position half reading `member JOIN-RAN of the write's value argument`.** **THIS IS THE EXACT HAZARD THE FILE FORBIDS FOR `hasOwnProperty` AT ITS OWN WORDS — *"no `hasOwnProperty` is taken from a mutable prototype"* (`:1136`–`:1137`, the rule restated at `:1656`–`:1658`) — and `NG-JS-Q17` is the same tree's own witness that the position limb takes `.join` from one.**

**(4b) THE RULING, WITH ITS GROUND: A DIAGNOSTIC-INTEGRITY BREACH AND NOT A BENIGN RESIDUAL, AND THEREFORE A HOST FIX.** Three grounds, none of them a preference: **(i) THE DATUM IS THE PACKAGE'S AND NOT THE VALUE'S.** The override residual exists because closing it needs a frozen prototype fact or an intrinsic-versus-override identity read, the thing `C-11` forbids — so the SUBJECT is conceded. **The POSITION is not a rendering of the value at all: it is the package's own diagnostic datum, computed from the walk's own path, and it can be spelled by a local loop THAT INVOKES NOTHING — so `C-11` never enters, and a concession taken here would be bought with nothing.** **(ii) THE POSITION IS ONE OF THE TWO FACTS A REFUSAL CARRIES (`§8` row `5`), and a caller's function standing where it belongs is the caller writing the package's diagnostic** — the same breach class the render guard was written to close, at the other half of the message. **(iii) THE FILE'S OWN RULE ALREADY FORBIDS THE FORM** (item `(4a)`), so a declared consequence would be an exception the file's own idiom denies without a ground. **AND THE RULING IS TAKEN HERE AND NOT DEFERRED: `LIVE-F18` recorded the state and referred its classification to the architect — it claims no verdict of its own and it is a live record, not a clause — and THIS pass is where the classification is stated: A DIAGNOSTIC-INTEGRITY BREACH, HOST FIX.**

**(4c) THE FIX, NAMED: a LOCAL INDEX LOOP over the path's segments and a LOCAL JOIN — `for (let i = 0; i < path.length; i += 1)` accumulating into a `let` string by `+=` — with the three segment renderings unmoved (an integer index to its bare decimal, `String(segment)`, `:1084`; a lone-surrogate key to `'<key: a string carrying a lone surrogate>'`, `:1091`; every other string key to `'<key>'`, `:1092`), and the `/` separator and the `member … of …` wrapper unmoved (`:1095`).** **NO PROTOTYPE METHOD IS INVOKED: no `map`, no `join`, no `forEach`, no `reduce`, and no array or string METHOD taken from a prototype — the form `Object.hasOwn` already takes over `hasOwnProperty` at the walk (`:1661`).**

**(4d) A CODE CHANGE IS OWED, RED-FIRST, AND ONE EXISTING CELL MOVES WITH IT — STATED PLAINLY BECAUSE IT IS THE ONE CELL THIS PASS FINDS ASSERTING THE CONTRARY OF THE LAW: `NG-JS-Q17` asserts the override's counter `2` and the marker as the position (`:5957`), and AFTER THE BYTE IT MUST ASSERT `1` AND THE DECLARED POSITION — its edit is the TEST WRITER'S and moves WITH the byte, never before it and never after it (`test-cases.md`'s clause of this date, item `(4)`).** **THE ACCEPTANCE CELL IS `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-59` — an overridden `Array.prototype.map` and an overridden `Array.prototype.join`, each counting and returning a marker, driven at the member-position refusal `write(addr, 'mem', {a: 10n})`, asserting the position `member 'a' of the write's value argument`, both counters `0`, the marker absent from every byte of the refusal, and the unmutated CONTROL byte-identical — the red FIRST and the byte SECOND (arm `(x)`).** **AND `LIVE-F18`'S END-STATE IS READ THROUGH THIS CLAUSE FROM THIS DATE: its second count and its `member JOIN-RAN …` position half are SUPERSEDED BY THE BYTE and are that row's own dated record until a live run measures the fixed tree; the live report is not edited here.**

**(4e) WHAT DOES NOT MOVE: the four segment forms and their exact bytes (`parameter-semantics.md §2`'s clause of `2026-10-24` on `F-RA-15`), the `/` join, the wrapper, the empty key's `''`, the non-injectivity declaration, the no-escaping declaration, the root spelling, every position cell of `§2`, `§8` row `5` and the class bullet.**

**(5) `F5-5` — THE CLAIM SENTENCE IS RE-DERIVED: IT NAMES THREE EXCEPTION LIMBS, AND A DONE ROW MAY QUOTE IT ONLY AT FULL LENGTH.**

**(5a) THE SENTENCE IT REPLACES, QUOTED SO A READER SEES WHAT MOVES — the `F-RA-22` clause's item `(b)`, verbatim: *no user code the caller supplied as the refused value's conversion path runs on a refusal path, except where that path lies on a prototype judged language-standard by shape at the two conditional arms AND IS AN OVERRIDDEN `toString`, `valueOf`, or — at an array's own `toString` — AN OVERRIDDEN `join`.*** **AND ITS ITEM `(d)`'S CLOSING CLAIM — *"the sentence's exception list is ONE family and NOTHING ELSE"* — with item `(e)`'s permission *"A DONE ROW MAY NOW QUOTE THE SENTENCE AT ITEM `(b)` WITHOUT A DATE QUALIFIER FOR `(ii)` OR `(iii)`"*. BOTH ARE TRUE OF THE WALK'S ARMS AND FALSE OF THE UNIT'S WHOLE REFUSAL SURFACE.**

**(5b) THE THREE PATHS THAT SURVIVE INSIDE THIS UNIT'S OWN REFUSAL SURFACE, EACH AT ITS HOME, SO NONE CAN BE DROPPED BY QUOTING A SHORTER SENTENCE:**
**(i) THE `(d)(i)` CARRIER SLOTS.** Their descriptor IS the value's own rendering (`targetSpelling`'s `String(value)` at `:1035`, with no render guard above it — the `try` at `:1021` and the `catch` at `:1036`–`:1037` answer a rendering that THROWS and cannot un-run a member that RETURNS), so an address carrying its own `toString` RUNS IT on a refusal path — LAWFULLY, under that clause's narrowed law — and that law lives in A DIFFERENT CLAUSE (`F-RA-18`'s items `(d)(i)` and `(d)(ii)` and the `JC-TC-55` clause's items `(1)` and `(2)`), which item `(e)`'s permission to quote item `(b)` ALONE drops.
**(ii) THE POSITION RENDERING.** Item `(4)`: until arm `(x)`'s byte lands, `valuePosition`'s `map`/`join` run a caller's overrides on EVERY member-position refusal (`LIVE-F18`'s measured second count). **THIS LIMB IS CLOSED BY AN OWED BYTE AND NOT BY A CONCESSION, so it is an exception UNTIL the byte lands and NO EXCEPTION from that date.**
**(iii) THE FURTHER READS OF THE SANCTIONED RENDERING ITSELF.** The exception the `DEF-2026-10-24-D` clause's item `(9)`'s sub-item `(e)` carried and `F-RA-22`'s item `(d)` declared DISCHARGED: item `(1c)` closed the element limb's CONVERSION MEMBERS, but the sanctioned `Array.prototype.toString` → `join` reaches each element by `[[Get]]`, while the element limb reads `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, i)` and `continue`s where the property is absent (`:1272`–`:1273`, its own comment *"a hole: `join` converts nothing there"*) — **SO AN INDEX CARRIED ONLY BY A PROTOTYPE IS NOT A HOLE TO `join`, AND AN INHERITED ACCESSOR AT THAT INDEX RUNS when an arm whose descriptor is the value's own rendering renders an array the walk never scanned: row `8`'s depth arm, and a `(d)(i)` carrier slot whose value is an array.** **This is a READ-FROM-BYTES claim and not a measured one; `F-RA-11`'s own shape `Object.defineProperty(Array.prototype, '0', { get() { … } })` is its witness, moved from the walked position to the depth arm, and NO CELL DRIVES IT.**
**(5c) THE SENTENCE FROM THIS DATE, EXACTLY, AND IT IS THE ONE A DONE ROW QUOTES: *no user code the caller supplied as the refused value's conversion path runs on a refusal path — except (i) where that path lies on a prototype judged language-standard by shape at the two conditional arms AND IS AN OVERRIDDEN `toString`, `valueOf`, or — at an array's own `toString` — AN OVERRIDDEN `join`; (ii) at a carrier position whose declared descriptor IS the value's own rendering, where that rendering runs the value's own `toString` because it is the descriptor; and (iii) through the further reads the sanctioned rendering itself performs, where an index carried only by a prototype is reached by `join`'s `[[Get]]` although the element limb's own-descriptor read cannot see it.***
**(5d) ITEM `(d)` RE-DERIVED AT THIS ITEM, BECAUSE A CLAUSE'S COUNT HAS NO CELL: *"the sentence's exception list is ONE family and NOTHING ELSE"* IS WITHDRAWN AT BOTH ITS COUNT AND ITS LIMIT and reads *"the sentence's exception list is THREE LIMBS AND NOTHING ELSE — the OVERRIDE family at the two conditional arms, the carrier positions whose declared descriptor is the value's own rendering, and the further reads the sanctioned rendering itself performs — and the position rendering is a FOURTH LIMB UNTIL ARM `(x)`'S BYTE LANDS AND NO LIMB FROM THAT DATE"*.** **AND THE CLAIMABILITY DATE IS DECLARED, because the sentence is exactly true only at a tree: THE SENTENCE IS CLAIMABLE FROM THE DATE ITEMS `(1)`, `(3)` AND `(4)`'S BYTES LAND — a DONE row written before then must append *"and except the position rendering, until its byte lands"* — and it is claimable AT FULL LENGTH ONLY: no DONE row may quote limb `(i)` alone, limbs `(i)` and `(ii)` alone, or item `(b)`'s text WITHOUT this sentence's three limbs.**
**(5e) WHAT DOES NOT MOVE: `§2.1` item `(1)`'s headline, item `(2)`'s three sanctioned renderings and six arms, the `F-RA-13` clause's items `(1a)`, `(1b)` and `(1c)`, the ten unconditional arms, every class spelling, position, status and `§2` row, and the residual's narrowest arm — `NG-JS-Q17`'s measured correction that an overridden `valueOf` alone does not run (`:5959`) — which limb `(i)`'s permissive wording already carries.**

**(6) `F5-6` — THE EXPIRED LIMIT IS WITHDRAWN AS A GROUND FOR AN UNWITNESSED STATE, AND THE `'OPMARK'` MEASUREMENT IS RESTATED AS A READ-FROM-BYTES CLAIM.**

**(6a) THE DEFECT, AT ITS BYTES: the `JC-TC-55` clause's item `(2)` justifies *"no cell is owed"* by quoting `F-RA-18`'s item `(f)` — *"Until the byte lands, NO CELL MAY ASSERT A SUBJECT AT A CARRIER POSITION WHOSE ADDRESS CARRIES A CONVERSION OR TAG MEMBER"* — AND THAT LIMIT IS TIME-SCOPED BY ITS OWN WORDS: it bounds itself to the landing of item `(f)`'s byte, and THE BYTE HAS LANDED (`:1354`, `if (renderGuardTrips(value)) return rowClass === undefined ? classSpelling(value) : rowClass;`).** **AN EXPIRED LIMIT MAY NOT BE THE REASON A STATE IS UNWITNESSED, and the same expiry reaches `test-cases.md`'s cell limit `(a)` of the `F-RA-18` clause, read through this item at that part's clause of this date.**
**(6b) THE WITNESS CENSUS, MEASURED AT THIS PASS'S READ AND FROM NO REPORT: the string `'OPMARK'` occurs in the tree at EXACTLY TWO SITES THAT PREDATE THIS CLAUSE — both inside this contract's own `JC-TC-55` clause (`:316` and `:317`) — and the further occurrences this clause writes are citations of those two; NO TEST FILE, NO GREENS CELL AND NO LIVE-REPORT ROW CARRIES IT, and no live-runner observation row records the counter `1`.** The measurement therefore has no run behind it.

No class, no status, no position, no `§2` row, no register row, no run figure and NO ADMISSION is re-decided by any item here; `§8`'s register rows `2`, `3` and `6` are outside this pass and stand byte-identical.**

**(1) `F6-1` — THE GUARD'S COST IS STILL CALLER-SCALABLE THROUGH THE PER-ELEMENT CHAIN WALK: THE BUDGET SPENDS POSITIONS AND ELEMENT-LIMB DESCRIPTOR READS AND NOTHING FOR THE CHAIN, SO THE LAW IT WAS RULED TO CLOSE IS NOT SATISFIED BY ITS OWN BYTE.**

**(1a) THE STATE, AT ITS BYTES AND MEASURED AT THIS PASS'S READ: `arrayElementsReachCallerCode` (`bindings/js/index.mjs:1325`) charges the array's own position (`:1332`), one position per index it reads (`:1345`) and a repeat's recorded count (`:1336`) — and then hands EVERY DATA ELEMENT to `renderGuardTrips` (`:1349`), whose entry (`:1385`) calls `carriesConversionMember(value)` (`:1387`, the reader at `:1214`) and `carriesTagMember(value)` (`:1388`, the reader at `:1256`) BEFORE the one limb that takes the allowance (`:1389`).** **NEITHER CHAIN READER TAKES AN ALLOWANCE AT ALL: each is one-argument and each walks its chain link by link to `null` with no charge anywhere — `carriesConversionMember`'s loop (`:1220`) reading one own descriptor per link for `Symbol.toPrimitive` (`:1223`), one shape judgement per link (`:1225`, `isLanguageStandardPrototype` `:1185`) and, above the exemption's stop, `toString`/`valueOf` (`:1229`, `ownsConversionMember` `:1172`); and `carriesTagMember`'s loop (`:1262`) reading one own descriptor per link (`:1264`).** **THE REFUSED VALUE'S OWN TWO WALKS ARE UNCHARGED AS WELL, `:1387` and `:1388` preceding every spend at `:1332`; and the spend primitive that would charge them already exists and is simply not reached from them — `spendGuardPosition` (`:1363`).**

**(1b) THE MULTIPLICATION THE FINDING NAMES, IN ARITHMETIC: `e` = the object at the head of a `K`-link prototype chain (`Object.create` applied `K` times, or a class's own chain), the refused value `arrayChain(new Array(M).fill(e), 127)` at row `8`'s DEPTH arm — the element limb then spends `M + 1` positions and performs `2·M·K`-scaled link visits, because EACH element is walked TWICE (the `@@toPrimitive` search and the tag search) and NO element's walk is charged.** **With `M = 10^4` and `K = 10^5` that is `~2×10^9` link visits against `~10^4` positions spent — two orders of magnitude of the one figure left unspent while the work runs several orders past any ceiling; and `M = 1` with `K = 10^8` is enough on its own, so the multiplication is caller-scalable in EITHER factor.** **AND THE GUARD NEED NOT TRIP FOR THE COST TO BE PAID: a chain carrying no conversion and no tag member answers "no caller code" at every link of every element, so the state is a SANCTIONED RENDERING whose price the caller sets.**

**(1c) THE LAW, OPERATIVE FROM THIS DATE AND IN ONE SENTENCE: EVERY READ THE RENDER GUARD PERFORMS IS CHARGED TO THE SAME ONE PER-CALL BUDGET — the array's own position, each index it reads, and EVERY LINK EVERY CHAIN WALK REACHES, per element and for the refused value itself — AND EXHAUSTION ANSWERS THE ARM'S CLASS SPELLING.** **A link is charged as one position WHEN THE WALK REACHES IT AND BEFORE THE READ THAT REACHED IT IS TAKEN, so no chain can be entered unpaid; the figure stays `NODE_CEILING` (`:1586`, `1,000,000`), the allowance stays created once per call and never per element and never at module level (`:1386`, and `:1323`'s comment is that rule's own statement at the byte), and NO SECOND FIGURE IS MINTED.** **THE CHARGE IS NOT A TRIP DECISION — exhaustion is the WITH branch's conservative answer, the class spelling, and never a class, a refusal state or a position, exactly as item `(1b)(iii)` and item `(1d)` of the `F5-1` clause rule — and the guard remains ONE conjunction whose answers no item here re-decides.**

**(1d) A CODE CHANGE IS OWED, RED-FIRST, AND ITS FORM IS NAMED SO THE IMPLEMENTER IS OWED BYTES: the allowance `arrayElementsReachCallerCode` already carries is threaded into the two chain readers — `carriesConversionMember(value, allowance)` and `carriesTagMember(value, allowance)` — each spending one position per link it enters (`:1220` · `:1262`) through `spendGuardPosition` (`:1363`) and answering "caller code is reachable" where the spend is refused; `renderGuardTrips` passes the same per-call `budget` it already creates (`:1386`) to both, at `:1387` and `:1388`, and each recursive hand-off at `:1349` keeps threading it, so NO CALL SITE IS LEFT UNCHARGED.** **NOTHING IS CAPTURED AND NOTHING IS CACHED BETWEEN CALLS — no census, no chain length, no memo keyed on an element — so `C-11` is untouched and the guard stays a per-call reader.** **THE RED CELL IS `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-60`, the red FIRST and the byte SECOND (`implementation-plan.md §2` step `4`'s clause of this date, arm `(xi)`); UNTIL THE BYTE LANDS no cell may assert this state's subject, no DONE row may claim the guard's work bounded per call, and `JC-TC-57`'s ceiling stays THAT cell's own and is not extended here.**

**(1e) THE OBSERVABLE, EXACTLY AS THE FINDING ASKS: A WITNESS WHOSE COST MULTIPLIES CHAIN TRAVERSALS ANSWERS THE DECLARED SUBJECT WITHIN THE WALL-CLOCK CEILING — `arrayChain(new Array(10_000).fill(e), 127)` with `e` carried on a `100,000`-link chain answers row `8`'s class spelling `a value over the walk ceiling` at the depth arm's own `127`-segment position and COMPLETES WITHIN `5` SECONDS; the CONTROL — the same `M` over a `2`-link chain carrying no conversion and no tag member — renders the refused array's own join, i.e. the join of `M` `'[object Object]'` spellings by `,`, byte-identically.** **THE CELL ASSERTS THE SUBJECT AND THE CEILING AND NO OTHER FIGURE, the link-visit count being bounded by the one budget as a matter of item `(1c)`'s law rather than by an instrumented counter; and `JC-TC-57`'s two states and five controls stand unmoved beside it.**

**(2) `F6-2` — `hasLoneSurrogate` TAKES `String.prototype.charCodeAt` FROM A MUTABLE PROTOTYPE AND IS THE SOLE GATE ON ROWS `9` AND `10`: A CALLER-SUPPLIED FUNCTION DECIDES BOTH FACTS, AN OVERRIDE ADMITS A LONE SURROGATE, AND A HOST FIX IS OWED.**

**(2a) THE BYTES, AND THE SOLE-GATE CLAIM MEASURED AT THIS PASS'S READ: `hasLoneSurrogate` (`bindings/js/index.mjs:683`) reads each code unit as `text.charCodeAt(i)` (`:685`) and a high surrogate's pair as `text.charCodeAt(i + 1)` (`:687`) — A MEMBER READ OFF `String.prototype` AT THE MOMENT OF THE CALL, so a replaced `String.prototype.charCodeAt` IS the code unit the predicate reads.** **AND NOTHING ELSE REFUSES THE CLASS: the walk's value arm refuses row `9` by this predicate and by no other read (`:1703`–`:1709`, the test at `:1706`), the walk's member loop refuses row `10` by it and by no other read (`:1823`, inside the loop at `:1810`), and the class has NO downstream refuser — the same predicate is the segment path's gate (`:1879`) and the constraint carrier's (`:2460`), and the file's own comment records what the predicate's absence produced: *"the as-built walk never called `hasLoneSurrogate` here, so `on(['\uD800'], f)` was admitted, `facade.rs`'s string path repaired the code unit to U+FFFD on the way in"* (`:1857`–`:1859`).** **SO AN OVERRIDE ANSWERING `0x41` AT EVERY INDEX MAKES ROWS `9` AND `10` ANSWER "NO LONE SURROGATE" AND THE VALUE OR THE KEY CROSSES — a value the store cannot spell in a Rust `String`, repaired silently on the far side, which is the one answer `§1`'s string bullet and rows `9`/`10` forbid.**

**(2b) THE RULING, IN ONE SENTENCE: THE PREDICATE READS THE STRING'S OWN CODE UNITS BY A LOCAL INDEX READ AND INVOKES NOTHING TAKEN FROM A PROTOTYPE, AND THAT IS A HOST FIX.** **THE FIRST OF THE TWO LAWFUL FORMS IS TAKEN, AND THE REASON IS THE ONE `C-11` GIVES: `const unit = text[i]` is a STRING'S OWN EXOTIC INDEX PROPERTY — the lookup is answered by the string wrapper's own character-index property, and the loop's bound `text.length` (`:684`) is itself the wrapper's own property, so EVERY index read is in range and NO LINK OF ANY PROTOTYPE CHAIN IS CONSULTED and no method is invoked — with the surrogate band decided by the language's own relational comparison against four single-code-unit literals, `'\uD800'` · `'\uDBFF'` · `'\uDC00'` · `'\uDFFF'`, so `'\uD800' <= unit && unit <= '\uDBFF'` is exactly `code >= 0xd800 && code <= 0xdbff` and the low-surrogate test is its mirror.** **AND THE PAIR READ CARRIES ITS OWN BOUND, WHICH IS PART OF THE FORM AND NOT AN OPTIMISATION: a high surrogate's second unit is read as `text[i + 1]` ONLY WHERE `i + 1 < text.length`, and where that index does not exist the answer is LONE — the same answer the shipped `text.charCodeAt(i + 1)` gave, because `!(NaN >= 0xdc00 && NaN <= 0xdfff)` is `true`. AN UNGUARDED `text[i + 1]` WOULD BREAK THE VERY PROPERTY THIS FORM IS TAKEN FOR: an out-of-range canonical index is not the wrapper's own property, so the lookup would go on to the chain and a caller's `String.prototype[i + 1] = '\uDC00'` would PAIR a high surrogate the caller never paired.** **THE SECOND FORM IS NAMED AND NOT TAKEN: `String.prototype.charCodeAt.call(text, i)` needs the intrinsic CAPTURED AT LOAD, and a captured prototype member is precisely the frozen prototype fact `C-11` forbids — the ground on which the `F-RA-13` clause's item `(6)` refused the pristine snapshot — while reading `String.prototype.charCodeAt` per call IS the defect.** **`C-11` IS THEREFORE UNTOUCHED FOR A REASON A READER CAN CHECK AT THE BYTES: the four boundary literals are ECMAScript's own surrogate band SPELLED IN THE FILE, the disposition `OBJECT_PROTO_STANDARD_NAMES` (`:712`) already takes for the standard member set; nothing is read from any prototype, at load or per call; and no realm's state is captured and no census is frozen.**

**(2c) CODE IS OWED AND THE RULING IS A HOST FIX, SAID PLAINLY WITH ITS WHY: the defect is a READ in the host file and not a clause's letter, so no clause can close it; and it is OWED rather than declaratory because an override defeats rows `9` AND `10` ENTIRELY — the class `a string carrying a lone surrogate` becomes unreachable at both positions, so an admission this contract forbids succeeds and is repaired on the far side.** **THE RED CELL IS `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-61`, the red FIRST and the byte SECOND (`implementation-plan.md §2` step `4`'s clause of this date, arm `(xii)`); UNTIL THE BYTE LANDS no cell may assert rows `9`/`10` as closed under a mutated `String.prototype`, and NO DONE ROW MAY QUOTE item `(1)`'s headline — *"a refusal's message is rendered from the walk's own facts, and NO USER CODE RUNS ON A REFUSAL PATH"* (`§2.1` item `(1)`, `:237`) — as a universal, the headline being false at one more state by this finding.**

**(2d) WHAT THIS ITEM DOES NOT MOVE, NAMED SO NOTHING IS SWEPT IN: rows `9` and `10` keep their class spelling `a string carrying a lone surrogate`, their positions (`value` and `key`) and their answer `ValueRefusedAtBoundary`; the per-arm table's row `9` and row `10` cells keep their subjects (`:222`–`:223`); the predicate's four other readings (`:1114` · `:1431` · `:1879` · `:2460`) keep their declared bytes and are read through this item for the FORM of the read alone; and THE CLAIM SENTENCE AT ITEM `(3)` IS NOT EXTENDED HERE — its subject is *the refused value's conversion path*, and a method of a language prototype is not that path, so no limb is added for it.** **AND THE CLASS `F5-4` RULED CLOSED IS NAMED AS RE-OPENED HERE IN ITS OWN TERMS: that clause's item `(4b)(iii)` holds that the file's own rule forbids the form — *"no `hasOwnProperty` is taken from a mutable prototype"* — and its item `(4b)` calls a caller's function standing where the package's datum belongs a DIAGNOSTIC-INTEGRITY BREACH AND A HOST FIX; this predicate is the same read at the ADMISSION half instead of the diagnostic half, and it is closed by a byte rather than conceded as a residual.**

**(3) `F6-3` — THE CLAIM SENTENCE'S LIMB `(ii)` IS OVER-STRONG: AT A `(d)(i)` SLOT THE RENDERING REACHES MORE THAN THE VALUE'S OWN `toString`, SO THE LIMB IS RE-DERIVED AND A DONE ROW MAY QUOTE THE SENTENCE ONLY AT FULL LENGTH.**

**(3b) THE FOUR MEMBERS, EACH WITH THE READ THAT REACHES IT: (i) `@@toPrimitive` — `ToPrimitive` consults it FIRST, by a `[[Get]]` that traverses the whole chain, and one the value supplies RUNS; (ii) `toString` — the first method `OrdinaryToPrimitive` takes at the string hint, whether own on the value or the `Object.prototype.toString` / `Array.prototype.toString` the chain supplies; (iii) `valueOf` — REACHED BEHIND THE VERY `toString` the withdrawn limb already conceded, because `OrdinaryToPrimitive` takes the second method where the first yields a non-primitive, which an own `toString` returning an object does; and (iv) `@@toStringTag` — read by `Object.prototype.toString`'s own `[[Get]]`, so `String({ [Symbol.toStringTag]: 'X' })` is `'[object X]'`, the read this file states for itself at `:1244`–`:1250`.** **THE FINDING NAMES TWO OF THE FOUR — `@@toPrimitive` and `@@toStringTag` — AND `valueOf` IS NAMED HERE FOR THE SAME REASON AND BY THE SAME TEST: the sentence must be exactly true at every `(d)(i)` slot, and a member reachable behind a member the limb already concedes cannot be omitted from an exception list whose whole work is to enumerate what may run.** **AND THE ONE `(d)(ii)` SLOT IS NOT THIS LIMB: `valueTargetSpelling`'s carrier slot (`:2452`) carries the render guard (`:1451`), which trips on exactly these members and answers the class spelling, so that slot's descriptor is a class spelling and no member runs at it.**

**(3c) THE SENTENCE FROM THIS DATE, EXACTLY, AND IT IS THE ONE A DONE ROW QUOTES AND ONLY AT FULL LENGTH: *no user code the caller supplied as the refused value's conversion path runs on a refusal path — except (i) where that path lies on a prototype judged language-standard by shape at the two conditional arms AND IS AN OVERRIDDEN `toString`, `valueOf`, or — at an array's own `toString` — AN OVERRIDDEN `join`; (ii) at a `(d)(i)` carrier position — a `targetSpelling` slot, which carries no render guard — whose declared descriptor IS the value's own rendering, where that rendering runs, because it is the descriptor, every member it reaches: the value's `@@toPrimitive` (the first lookup `ToPrimitive` makes, a `[[Get]]` that traverses the chain), its `toString` (the first method `OrdinaryToPrimitive` takes at the string hint), its `valueOf` (reached where that `toString` yields a non-primitive), and its `@@toStringTag` (read by `Object.prototype.toString`'s own `[[Get]]`); and (iii) through the further reads the sanctioned rendering itself performs, where an index carried only by a prototype is reached by `join`'s `[[Get]]` although the element limb's own-descriptor read cannot see it.***

**(3d) WHAT MOVES AND WHAT DOES NOT, ENUMERATED SO NOTHING ELSE IS RE-DERIVED BY IMPLICATION: limb `(ii)`'s LETTER and nothing else — limbs `(i)` and `(iii)` stand byte-identical, item `(5d)`'s count stands (the exception list is THREE LIMBS AND NOTHING ELSE) and its own name for this limb (*"the carrier positions whose declared descriptor is the value's own rendering"*) still names it, item `(5b)(i)`'s prose (*"an address carrying its own `toString` RUNS IT on a refusal path"*) is TRUE AND NOT EXHAUSTIVE and is read through this item for the member list, item `(5b)(iii)`'s further reads stand, and item `(5d)`'s claimability date and full-length rule are unmoved.** **NO CODE IS OWED BY THIS FINDING AND NO CELL MOVES: the sentence is a letter about a law whose bytes are the renderer's own (`:1020`–`:1052`), and `JC-TC-55`'s and `JC-TC-58`'s drives stand as filed.**

**(4) `F6-4` — THE TWO PER-ARM WITH LISTS OMIT THE GUARD'S EXHAUSTION PATH, SO A READER PREDICTING A SUBJECT AT THOSE ARMS FROM THE TABLE ALONE IS WRONG: THE DISJUNCT IS ADDED IN PLACE AT BOTH CELLS.**

**(4a) THE STATE, AT THE TABLE'S BYTES: row `3`'s `<subject>` cell (`:215`) lists the function's own source text, the class spelling `'a function'` where *"that rendering throws"* or where *"the function carries such an own member, or carries an own or added `Symbol.toStringTag` member"*, and `'a Proxy'` for a callable `Proxy`; row `8`'s DEPTH-arm cell (`:220`) lists the value's own spelling and the class spelling where the value carries one, *"or carries an own or added `Symbol.toStringTag` member, or is an ARRAY whose element reads trip the guard"*. **⟶ AMENDED 2026-10-23 — THE GATE-3 PROOFREADING PASS (`role_proofreader`; `RCA-8(g)`): THE REFUSAL OBJECT'S MEMBER SET IS `{status, message, log}`, AND `docs/specs/storeroom-js/surface.md §2.5` IS THE NORMATIVE DECLARATION OF IT.** **The clause above stands byte-for-byte and is read THROUGH this one; THIS clause is a PURE INSERTION, it withdraws no rule of it, and the ONE FORM, the no-second-form prohibition, the no-raw-host-error prohibition and the no-side-channel rule all stand unmoved.** **WHICH DOCUMENT IS NORMATIVE, AND ON WHAT AUTHORITY:** the member set of the crossed refusal is declared at `docs/specs/storeroom-js/surface.md §2.5` — that section is the JS surface's own single-failure-form clause, and its clause of 2026-10-16 re-derived the form from two members to three on the architect's rulings *"Override the failure form limit"* and *"Bubble the message log to the client on failure, override any clauses that forbid this"*; its descriptor item fixes the third member's shape in terms no other document repeats: `log` is an ORDINARY OWN ENUMERABLE DATA MEMBER of the same plain object — written with an object literal, never `Object.defineProperty`, never a getter, never on a prototype, never behind a `Symbol` — so `Object.keys(caught)` reads exactly `["log","message","status"]` and its length is `3` ON EVERY ARM, and *"`log` IS PRESENT ON EVERY FAILURE THIS SURFACE THROWS, AND `[]` IS ITS DECLARED VALUE WHERE THE FAILURE'S CAUSE HAS NO ATTEMPT AT ALL"*. **`{status, message}` IN THE CLAUSE ABOVE IS THEREFORE READ AS `{status, message, log}` — A STALE ARITY AND NOT A SECOND FORM — and no other byte of it moves.** **THE MEASURED CENSUS AGREES:** `bindings/js/index.mjs:590`'s `fail` throws `{ status, message, log: refusalLog(log) }`, which is the object `boundaryReject` (`:655`, reaching `fail` at `:669`) produces for every refusal of this contract, so the crossing's own refusal's census is the three members that section declares. **AND THE THIRD MEMBER CHANGES NOTHING OF THIS CONTRACT'S SUBSTANCE:** `log` is neither the class nor the position — `surface.md §2.5`'s verbose item, clause (b), which carries *"carried INSIDE the generated message's own text and NEVER as a third member"*, is read through that section's 2026-10-16 pointer as refusing a TARGET-OR-REASON member, which `log` is not — so the class-and-position clause stays inside PART TWO's own text, `MessageArgs` gains no member (`§2.1`'s provenance paragraph, unmoved), the status stays `ValueRefusedAtBoundary` at rank `17`, and on this route the arms are the `log: []` ones. **THE DRIFT THIS DISCHARGES:** the blind set recorded the two-member reading as a declared drift and owed its reconciliation to the pass owning the form (`docs/specs/storeroom-json-crossing-greens.md` `§2.2` item `1` and `§3.4`); this clause IS that reconciliation, its norm is `surface.md §2.5`, and the member census it records is the one the blind set measured. `[A2 docs/decisions.md · `A148`; A3 docs/specs/storeroom-js/surface.md §2.5 — its clause of 2026-10-16, items 1–10, and its amendment of 2026-10-23 | requirement]`

**⟶ AMENDED 2026-10-24 — THE GATE-11 SEVENTH AUDIT PASS (read-only, `2026-10-24`; findings `F7-1` · `F7-2` · `F7-3`; `RCA-8(g)`): THE CROSSING DECLARES ONE SYSTEMATIC LAW FOR EVERY METHOD, ITERATOR, ITERATION PROTOCOL AND COERCION TAKEN FROM A MUTABLE PROTOTYPE ON ITS ADMISSION, REFUSAL, GUARD, POSITION-RENDERING AND COPY PATHS — WITH ITS POSITIVE FORM AND AN INVENTORY OF EVERY REMAINING SITE BY SYMBOL AND MEASURED LINE; THE RENDER GUARD'S COST UNIT IS DECLARED PER READ AND ITS CODE IS OWED RED-FIRST; THE CLAIM SENTENCE IS RECORDED AT ITS EXACT BOUNDARY AND GAINS ITS SECOND SENTENCE; THE TWO OVER-STRONG HEADLINES ARE RE-DERIVED; AND THREE RESIDUES ARE PARKED WITH THEIR OWNERS.**
**Every clause of `§1`, `§2` and this section above stands byte-for-byte and is read THROUGH this one, EXCEPT the two headlines item `(5)` re-derives at their own numbers and the one sentence item `(6)` re-derives at its own; no class, no status, no position, no message byte, no `§2` row, no `§8` register row, no run figure and no admission is re-decided here, `§8`'s rows `2`, `3` and `6` are outside this pass and stand byte-identical, and the status line above stands byte-for-byte because this clause changes no gate status.** **THIS CLAUSE IS PLACED AT `§2.1` AND NOT AT `§2` — the placement is stated so a reader need not infer it: its subject is the GUARD and the RENDERING as much as the walk, the three rulings of this class that already stand are here (`F5-4`'s position spelling, `F6-2`'s lone-surrogate predicate, `F6-1`'s budget), and a law whose enforcement instruments are the battery of item `(2)` and the cost unit of item `(4)` has no home at `§2`, whose subject is the seventeen refusal classes. ITS REACH IS NOT CONFINED BY THAT PLACEMENT: items `(1g)`, `(2)` and `(3)` name the `§1` census sites, the `§2` refusal sites and the `§5` copy sites it governs, so no reader takes a section boundary for the law's boundary. NO DEFECT ROW IS FILED BY THIS CLAUSE — the three findings are named `F7-1`–`F7-3` from this audit pass and a `DEF` id is a tracker edit outside this pass's scope.**

**(1) `F7-1` — THE LAW: ONE CLASS, DECLARED ONCE, WITH ITS POSITIVE FORM.**

**(1a) THE CLASS, AND WHY IT IS DECLARED ONCE.** **Six gate-11 audits have closed THE SAME DEFECT CLASS five times, one site at a time:** `i in value` at the walk's hole scan; `Array.prototype.map` at the position limb; `String.prototype.charCodeAt` at the lone-surrogate predicate; an own-descriptor scan's `forEach`-style iteration; and now — measured at the seventh audit — **`Array.prototype.includes` at the ancestor test (`:1783`) and the census arrays' ITERATION PROTOCOL at rows `13`, `14`, `15` and `17` (`:815` · `:825` · `:886` · `:926` · `:974` · `:1840` · `:1866`). EACH FIX WAS CORRECT AND EACH LEFT THE NEXT SITE, because no clause stated what a LAWFUL operation looks like — so this clause states the law and enumerates the sites, and the next implementer pass SWEEPS an inventory instead of hunting a symptom.**

**(1b) THE OPERATIVE SENTENCE, IN ONE LINE, AND IT IS THE LAW: *no method, iterator, iteration protocol, or coercion taken from a mutable prototype is invoked anywhere on the crossing's admission, refusal, guard, position-rendering or copy path, and no such member is captured at load either — every such operation is a local index loop, an `Object.hasOwn` or another `Object` static, an own-descriptor read, or a spelled relational comparison.***

**(1c) THE POSITIVE FORM, ENUMERATED SO A NEW SITE IS CHECKABLE WITHOUT RE-DERIVING THE CLASS. An operation on these five paths is LAWFUL in exactly these forms and in no other:** **(i) A LOCAL INDEX LOOP** — `for (let i = 0; i < x.length; i += 1)`, where `x.length` is the datum's OWN property (an array's exotic `length`, a string's `length`, a census array's `length`) and the element is `x[i]`; **(ii) AN `Object` STATIC'S RESULT** — `Object.hasOwn`, `Object.getOwnPropertyNames`, `Object.getOwnPropertySymbols`, `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor`, `Object.getPrototypeOf`, `Object.keys`, `Object.entries`, `Object.fromEntries`, `Object.is`, `Object.create`, `Object.defineProperty`, `Array.isArray`, `Number.isInteger`, `Number.isFinite`, `Number.isSafeInteger` and `util.types.*` — **the CALL is lawful and the RESULT'S ITERATION is not, which is the inventory's most common `OWED` row**; **(iii) AN OWN-DESCRIPTOR READ** — `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(x, key)`, which invokes no getter, and the descriptor object's own `get`/`set`/`value` members, which are own data properties of a spec-minted object; **(iv) A SPELLED RELATIONAL COMPARISON** — `===`, `!==`, `<`, `>`, `<=`, `>=` against a literal or another own datum, the form `hasLoneSurrogate` takes for its two surrogate bands; **(v) AN OWN EXOTIC INDEX READ** — `text[i]` on a string and `array[i]` on a genuine array, each answered by the object itself and by no link of any chain; **(vi) A MEMBERSHIP TEST WRITTEN AS A LOCAL LOOP** — the form that replaces every `Set` and `Map` membership in item `(2)`.

**⟶ CORRECTED 2026-10-25 — THE WRITE FORM, DECLARED BESIDE ITEM `(1c)`, AND THE READING THAT MADE AN APPEND BY INDEX LAWFUL IS SUPERSEDED BY NAME (`F8-2`'s copy-OUT vector; `RCA-8(g)`: a clause that prescribes a defect is a defect).** **WHAT WAS READ, AND IT IS KEPT BYTE-FOR-BYTE ABOVE: item `(1c)` enumerates the LAWFUL READS of the five paths and states NO WRITE FORM AT ALL — while two clauses of this section name `out[out.length] = …` AS IF IT WERE ONE, and it is not: item `(2)`'s inventory row `55` PRESCRIBES it as the replacement for `crossTriggered`'s `out.push(…)`, and the counter-narrowing clause's item `(1a)` reads it in the then-shipped bytes of `crossTriggered` (`:3148`–`:3150`).** **THE READING THAT IS SUPERSEDED: that a local index loop's APPEND is lawful because a local index loop's READ is. `out[out.length] = …` is a `[[Set]]` and `[[Set]]` WALKS THE PROTOTYPE CHAIN, so under an own getter-only accessor at `Array.prototype[0]` the append answers `false`, leaves the index a HOLE, and in a module frame raises a raw host `TypeError` — measured at this class's write vector (`tests/e2e/prototype-index-write.test.mjs`) and, on the copy-OUT side, as a WRONG VALUE out of a lawful call rather than a refusal (`tests/e2e/prototype-index-array-out.test.mjs`: a write of `['x','y']` answered the prototype's own answer at each index).**
**(1) THE LAWFUL WRITE FORMS, FOR THE JS HALF, ARE EXACTLY THREE: (a) AN ARRAY LITERAL; (b) `Array.of(…)`; (c) DEFINING AN OWN PROPERTY — `Object.defineProperty`, or the package's own `appendOwn` (`bindings/js/src/entry/refusal.mjs`), which is the form every append on a governed path takes in the shipped bytes.** **`out[out.length] = …` and `Array.prototype.push` are NOT lawful writes on any of item `(1g)`'s five paths from this date; a LOCAL INDEX LOOP remains the lawful READ form and is unmoved.**
**(2) AND THE NATIVE EQUIVALENT, FOUND WHERE THE SAME CLASS DID THE SAME WORK: a value the codec builds for a caller is built by a call that DEFINES an own property and never by one that SETS it — `napi_define_properties` with one `napi_property_descriptor` per index (or per member), over an array created at its own `length`; never `napi_create_array_with_length` + `napi_set_element`, and never `napi_set_property`/`napi_set_named_property`.** **The copy-OUT sites are `bindings/js/src/codec.rs`'s `fn array` (closed from this date) and `fn set_member` (closed beside it); `fn object`'s `Object::new` + `define_properties` was already this form.** **A REFUSAL IS NOT THE ALTERNATIVE AND IS NOT AVAILABLE: no clause of this contract names a refusal for a value the copy OUT cannot build, so refusing would mint a class the closed census of `§2` does not declare.**
**(3) NOTHING ELSE MOVES: item `(1b)`'s law, item `(1c)`'s six forms, item `(1d)`'s boundary, item `(1e)`'s three grounds, item `(2)`'s inventory and every marking, row `55`'s symbol, family, marking and revisit condition, every `§2` class, position, subject and status, and every other figure of this section stand as filed.** **The only bytes withdrawn are the APPEND HALF of row `55`'s remedy cell — read as item `(1c)(i)`'s index loop, which stands, plus form `(1)(c)` above where a list must GROW — and the descriptive APPEND HALF of the counter-narrowing clause's item `(1a)`; both are read THROUGH this clause.**

**(1d) THE BOUNDARY OF THE LAW, STATED SO A SWEEP DOES NOT OVER-REACH — AND IT IS NOT A LOOPHOLE, IT IS WHERE THE PROTOTYPE ENDS. The law governs PROTOTYPE MEMBERS, ITERATORS, ITERATION PROTOCOLS AND COERCIONS. It does NOT govern:** **(i) a global constructor binding** (`Object`, `Array`, `Number`, `String`, `Set`, `Map`) — a binding is not a prototype, and a pass that also froze the bindings would be a second law this clause does not declare, so a sweep STOPS AT THE PROTOTYPES; **(ii) a constructor's STATIC** — lawful, and named so by item `(1c)(ii)`, which is why `Object.hasOwn` is the declared replacement for `Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty` and not a second hole; **(iii) a WELL-KNOWN SYMBOL VALUE read off the `Symbol` constructor** — `Symbol.toPrimitive` and `Symbol.toStringTag` are not prototype members and do not change, so `CONVERSION_MEMBER_KEYS`'s load-time array of them (`:1188`) is lawful as a VALUE and only its ITERATION is owed (row `33`); **(iv) an ALLOCATION** — `new Set()` and `new Map()` with no iterable argument read nothing off a prototype, while the INSTANCE'S METHODS (`has`, `add`, `get`, `set`, `size`) and the CONSTRUCTOR'S ITERATION OF AN ARGUMENT are prototype and iteration reads respectively; **(v) an own member read of a value the walk has already classified** — `value[name]` on an admitted plain object reads an own data property, and where the member could be an accessor the walk has already refused the value (row `15`, `:926`–`:928`).

**(1e) THE GROUND, THREE PARTS, EACH DECISIVE ON ITS OWN.** **(i) A CALLER'S ONE-LINER MUST NOT DECIDE A VERDICT.** A verdict is the crossing's own answer about a value's class; a prototype member is the caller's function, so a verdict that turns on one is a caller-supplied answer wearing the package's status — item `(1f)`'s two measurements. **(ii) `C-11`'s NO-FROZEN-PROTOTYPE-FACT RULE FORBIDS THE OTHER DIRECTION.** A captured intrinsic is a fact about the prototype frozen at load, and the gate-4 adversarial pass measured what that costs (a later own-property change to the prototype left every array address and every plain `cfg` comparing against a key set the realm no longer had, so `[]` and `{}` were refused); **that is why the alternative form `String.prototype.charCodeAt.call(...)` — which invokes a known function and never consults a caller's override — is NOT lawful here: `C-11` is honoured by NOT CAPTURING AT LOAD, so the lawful forms must be READ-PER-CALL AND MEMBER-FREE, and no clause of this contract may close this class by capturing an intrinsic without re-opening `C-11` first.** **(iii) THE DIRECTION A FAILURE MUST TAKE IS THE REFUSAL.** Where a read the package needs cannot be taken in a lawful form, the answer is the row's CLASS SPELLING — the fail-closed direction the `F5-1` clause's item `(1d)` already declares — and never a caller's answer.

**(1f) THE TWO MEASURED CONSEQUENCES THE FINDING NAMES, AT THEIR BYTES.**
**(i) `Array.prototype.includes` AT THE ANCESTOR TEST (`:1783`) DECIDES ROW `11`.** With `Array.prototype.includes = () => true`, `write(addr, 'mem', {a: 1})` refuses an **ACYCLIC** value as `a value that contains itself` at the root `value` position — a caller's one-liner decides a REFUSAL, and the refused value's conversion path is reached at NEITHER that answer (which hands the class spelling, `refusedAsClass` `:1681`) NOR the guard, whose own path test (`:1378`) trips under the same override and so WITHDRAWS the sanctioned `'1,2'` / `'[object Object]'` renderings. **AND THE OTHER DIRECTION IS WORSE, WHICH IS WHY THE LAW IS NOT A TIDINESS RULE: with `Array.prototype.includes = () => false` a genuine CYCLE is not detected, the walk recurses at `:1851`/`:1887` with no `try` anywhere in `encodeValue` (`:1698`–`:1894`), and a RAW HOST `RangeError` escapes the package — the one form `docs/specs/storeroom-js/surface.md §2.5` item 5 (e) and `A76` forbid.**
**(ii) THE CENSUS ARRAYS' ITERATION PROTOCOL DECIDES ROWS `13`, `14`, `15` AND `17`.** With a forged `Array.prototype[Symbol.iterator]`, `Object.create(Object.create(null))` **crosses as a plain object** — `isStandardObjectPrototype` (`:872`) reads the value's prototype's census as `new Set(Object.getOwnPropertyNames(proto))` (`:886`), the `Set` constructor consumes that census THROUGH THE FORGED ITERATOR, the carrying test at `:891` then passes for a prototype that owns NOTHING, and `protoClass` (`:917`) answers `'foreign'` — while `const a = [1]; a.foo = 3` **never tests `foo`**, because row `17`'s only gate is `for (const key of Object.keys(value))` (`:1840`) and the member loop is `for (const name of Object.keys(value))` (`:1866`), so an array carrying a NON-INDEX OWN MEMBER crosses as `[1]` with that member silently DROPPED. **AND THE SAME FORGE REACHES ROW `15`'S ACCESSOR TEST, WHICH IS THE ONE ADMISSION-PATH READ THAT RUNS CALLER CODE: `accessorOrSymbolArm`'s scan is `for (const name of Object.getOwnPropertyNames(value))` (`:926`), so a skipped name leaves an own ACCESSOR untested and the member loop then reads `value[name]` (`:1887`) — CALLER CODE RUNS ON AN ADMISSION PATH, which is the direction item `(1e)(i)` forbids and no cell of the landed set drives.**

**(1g) THE FIVE PATHS THE LAW GOVERNS, EACH NAMED BY ITS ENTRY SYMBOL SO NO PATH IS LEFT TO INFERENCE:** **(i) the ADMISSION path** — `encodeValue` (`:1698`) and everything it calls: `hasLoneSurrogate` (`:702`), `arrayClass` (`:960`), `protoClass` (`:917`), `isStandardObjectPrototype` (`:872`), `referenceSet` (`:852`), `plainProtoNames` (`:814`), `arrayProtoNames` (`:824`), `arrayProtoSymbols` (`:837`), `accessorOrSymbolArm` (`:925`), `isCanonicalArrayIndex` (`:1692`); **(ii) the REFUSAL path** — `valueReject` (`:1552`), `refusedValue` (`:1666`), `refusedAsClass` (`:1681`), `classSpelling` (`:1017`), `shapeArmClass` (`:1563`); **(iii) the GUARD** — `renderGuardTrips` (`:1441`), `arrayElementsReachCallerCode` (`:1373`), `carriesConversionMember` (`:1250`), `carriesTagMember` (`:1302`), `ownsConversionMember` (`:1194`), `isLanguageStandardPrototype` (`:1207`), `spendGuardPosition` (`:1411`); **(iv) the POSITION RENDERING** — `valuePosition` (`:1128`), its lone-surrogate key reading (`:1136`), `spellConstraintAddress` (`:2530`), `boundaryReject`'s container spelling (`:665`); **(v) the COPY path** — `encodeValue`'s member construction (`:1845`–`:1893`) and `decodeValue` (`:2582`), `decodeSegments` (`:2631`). **A site on none of these five is NOT this clause's subject, and item `(3)` says which sites those are and who owns them.**

**(2) THE INVENTORY — EVERY REMAINING SITE, BY SYMBOL AND MEASURED LINE, WITH A CLOSED MARKING ENUM. This is the pass's deliverable: the next implementer pass sweeps this table, so a site absent from it is a site the law will find later.** **⟶ RE-SPELLED 2026-10-24 (`RCA-8(g)`; `§2.1`'s clause of this date on the counter narrowing, `F8-8`, item `(4)`): *"EVERY REMAINING SITE"* IS READ AS *EVERY SITE IN `bindings/js/index.mjs` ON THE SIX PATHS OF ITEM `(1g)`, PLUS EVERY `ROUTED` FAMILY AS A ROW OF ITS OWN WITH ITS OWNER AND ITS REVISIT CONDITION* — BYTE-FOR-BYTE ABOVE AND READ THROUGH THIS READING — because the absolute was measured FALSE by the eighth audit (`F8-1`'s item `(1c)`: the record-copy and delivery family and the argument and construction family stood in this file and were absent from this table), and the two limbs of that clause's item `(4b)` are the claim that replaces it.**
