`S2` — every census ARRAY handed to `new Set(...)`, replaced by the census array ITSELF, kept and read by index (rows `1` · `2` · `8` · `11`, `4` sites); `S3` — every `Set.prototype` method, replaced by item `(1c)(vi)`'s membership loop over the two census arrays (rows `3` · `6` · `7` · `9` · `10` · `12` · `13` · `14`, `8` sites); `S4` — `Symbol.prototype.description`'s accessor, replaced by a descriptor read of the symbol's own `description` data member (row `4`, `1` site, and row `12`'s `.description` half is the same form); `S5` — the allowance's `Map`, replaced by a local pair list read by index (rows `30` · `32`, `2` sites); `S6` — `Array.prototype.push`, replaced by `x[x.length] = …` (rows `22` · `24` · `43` · `45` · `47`, `5` sites); `S7` — `Array.prototype.includes`, replaced by a local loop (rows `18` · `29` · `54`, `3` sites); `S8` — `BigInt.prototype.toString`, replaced by `String(value)` on the bigint primitive (row `26`, `1` site); `S9` — the two `.map`/`.join` DIAGNOSTIC spellings, replaced by item `(1c)(i)`'s loop with `+=` (rows `39` · `40`, `2` sites). **`11 + 4 + 8 + 1 + 2 + 5 + 3 + 1 + 2 = 37` ✓, AND THE OVERLAPS ARE DECLARED SO NO READER DERIVES `37` FROM A MEMBER LIST: rows `22` and `24` each take TWO members (`push` and a spread) and are counted ONCE, in `S6`, their spread half being `S1`'s form; row `12` is counted once, in `S3`, its `.description` half being `S4`'s form; row `54` is counted in `S7` although its owner may be another unit's (its row says so).**

**(3) THE SCOPE BOUNDARY, THE ROUTED SITES, AND WHAT THE LAW DOES NOT REACH.**

**(3a) THE FIVE `ROUTED` ROWS ARE NAMED AND ARE NOT CLAIMS OF CLOSURE.** Each is outside item `(1g)`'s five paths; **`ROUTED` MEANS "NOT THIS CLAUSE'S SUBJECT AND NOT OWED HERE", never "lawful"** — the marking enum's own definitions say so — and a reader who needs one of them takes it to its owner named in the row.** **(3b) `:4226`'S `i in address` IS RECORDED AS ROUTED AND NOT OWED HERE, EXACTLY AS THE FINDING ASKS.** It stands in `inDomainAddress` (`:4223`), an ADDRESS reader; `§1`'s subject caption excludes carrier addresses and their segment domain; and `§2`'s clause of `2026-10-24` on `F-RA-11`, item `(8)`, already routes the three carrier-position `in` tests — `encodeAddress`'s hole scan (`:2172`), `constraintAddress`'s (`:2512`) and this one (`:4226`) — by the same ground. **NO CLAUSE OF THIS PASS SWEEPS ANY OF THE THREE, and row `49`'s re-pointed lines supersede the figures `F-RA-11` printed (`:1867` · `:2207` · `:3921`).** **(3c) THE SAME CLASS OUTSIDE THE CAPTION IS NAMED SO IT IS NOT MISTAKEN FOR CLOSED:** the absent-addon message's `.map`/`.join` (row `51`), the registry's `.replace` (row `52`) and the two registry key joins (row `53`) are the same defect class at paths this contract's caption does not govern; **they are ROUTED to this unit's next diagnostic pass and to the registry's own owner, and a pass that closes them closes them under its own clause and not under this law.** **(3d) THE ONE ROW WHOSE OWNERSHIP MAY SPLIT** is `54` (`constraintCrossing`'s `KC_TOKENS.includes`): it is on a REFUSAL path inside this file and it is the constraints unit's fail surface; this clause OWES it and says in its row that the sweep must not double-count it if the other unit's pass claims it first.**

**(4) `F7-2` — THE COST UNIT: ARM `(xi)` CHARGES CHAIN LENGTH AND NOT CHAIN COST, SO "THE RENDER GUARD IS BOUNDED" IS NOT YET TRUE AS A CLAIM.**

**(4a) THE STATE, AT ITS BYTES AND MEASURED AT THIS PASS'S READ.** Arm `(xi)` HAS LANDED: `carriesConversionMember` (`:1250`) spends one position per link at `:1259` and `carriesTagMember` (`:1302`) at `:1310`, both threaded from the one per-call allowance. **BUT ONE POSITION PER LINK IS NOT THE LINK'S COST.** A link entered at `:1256` is then read by: **one `Symbol.toPrimitive` descriptor read (`:1262`); one SHAPE JUDGEMENT (`:1264` → `isLanguageStandardPrototype` `:1207` → `isStandardObjectPrototype` `:872` → one `getPrototypeOf` (`:885`) and ONE CENSUS READ (`:886`) whose length is the LINK'S OWN NAME COUNT, plus a `Set` built member by member and a carrying loop over the reference set (`:891`)); and, above the exemption's stop, three more descriptor reads (`:1268` → `ownsConversionMember` `:1195`).** **THE CENSUS READ IS THE TERM NO CHARGE REACHES, AND IT IS CALLER-SCALED IN LENGTH: a terminal prototype carrying `N` own names costs `N + |reference| + 3` reads per visit and is charged ONE POSITION for the link.** **(4b) THE ARITHMETIC, PRINTED SO THE FINDING IS CHECKABLE.** Let `proto` be an `Object.create(null)` carrying the standard `Object.prototype` member set (so `isLanguageStandardPrototype` answers TRUE and the exemption stops the walk at that one link) PLUS `100,000` further own data names, and let `e = Object.create(proto)`; refuse `arrayChain(new Array(10_000).fill(e), 127)` at row `8`'s DEPTH arm. **EACH element then costs `~100,012` reads and is charged `4` positions — the array's own position (`:1380`), the index read (`:1393`) and one link entry per chain reader (`:1259` · `:1310`) — so `M = 10,000` elements cost `~10^9` READS AGAINST `~30,000` POSITIONS SPENT, and the guard never trips: the multiplication the finding names is `M × N` and only `M` is charged. AND THE COST IS PAID WITHOUT A TRIP: the chain carries no conversion and no `Symbol.toStringTag` member, so the arm is a SANCTIONED RENDERING whose price the caller sets.** **(4c) THE COST UNIT, DECLARED — AND THIS IS THE RULING: ONE POSITION PER READ, AND A READ OF A CALLER-SCALED LIST IS CHARGED ITS OWN LENGTH.** **A READ IS, EXHAUSTIVELY: (i) one `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor` call — `1`; (ii) one `Object.getPrototypeOf` call — `1`; (iii) one own-NAME census read — `1 + the length of the list it returns`, one for the call and ONE PER MEMBER, so a `100,000`-name census costs `100,001`; (iv) one own-SYMBOL census read — the same `1 + length`; (v) one element index read — `1`; (vi) one link entered — `1`, charged on entry and before the first read of that link, so NO CHAIN IS ENTERED UNPAID; (vii) one memo hit — THE REPEAT'S RECORDED COUNT, the reading the `F5-1` clause's item `(1b)(iii)` already declares; (viii) an ALLOCATION, a `typeof`, an identity compare and a spelled relational comparison — `0`, being neither caller-scaled nor a member read.** **NO SECOND FIGURE IS MINTED: the budget stays `NODE_CEILING` (`:1642`, `1,000,000`), it is created once per call of `renderGuardTrips` (`:1441`, `:1442`) and never per element and never at module level, and EXHAUSTION IS NOT A TRIP DECISION — it is the WITH branch's conservative answer, the arm's class spelling, exactly as the `F5-1` clause's item `(1b)(iii)` and item `(1d)` rule. The guard remains ONE conjunction whose answers no item here re-decides.** **(4d) THE DECLARED CONSEQUENCE, STATED SO IT IS NOT FILED AS A DEFECT: once the unit is a READ, `1,000,000` positions buys fewer links than it does today — a chain whose links each carry a `41`-name census exhausts after about `22,000` links — and a refused value at row `3` or row `8`'s depth arm whose READ cost exceeds the figure answers its CLASS SPELLING where the shipped guard would have rendered it. THAT IS THE FAIL-CLOSED DIRECTION ITEM `(1e)(iii)` DECLARES, and NO CELL MAY ASSERT SUCH A STATE AS A RENDERING.** **(4e) A CODE CHANGE IS OWED, RED-FIRST, AND ITS FORM IS NAMED SO THE IMPLEMENTER IS OWED BYTES: a `spendGuardRead(allowance, count)` beside `spendGuardPosition` (`:1411`), and every census read charged through it — `isStandardObjectPrototype`'s `:886`, `arrayClass`'s `:974` and their two symbol censuses (`:840` · `:983`) — with the allowance threaded into `isStandardObjectPrototype`, `arrayClass`, `referenceSet`, `plainProtoNames`, `arrayProtoNames`, `arrayProtoSymbols` and `isLanguageStandardPrototype`, each answering the WITH branch's conservative answer where the spend is refused, and `NODE_CEILING` UNMOVED.** **THE RED CELL IS `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-62` — item `(4b)`'s witness against its control — the red FIRST and the byte SECOND (`implementation-plan.md §2` step `4`'s clause of this date, arm `(xvii)`); UNTIL THE BYTE LANDS no cell may assert that witness's subject, and item `(5)`'s headline is the only claimable form.**

**(5) THE TWO OVER-STRONG HEADLINES, RE-DERIVED.**

**(5a) HEADLINE `1`, QUOTED AND RE-DERIVED: `F5-1`'s clause of `2026-10-24` item `(1h)` reads *"THE UNIT MAY NOT CLAIM THAT ITS RENDER GUARD IS BOUNDED"*, AND THAT HEADLINE IS STILL TRUE — but it is now true for a NEW reason and it must not be read as satisfied by arm `(xi)`'s landing.** Item `(1h)`'s own condition was arm `(viii)`'s byte; **that byte stands (`:1380` · `:1384` · `:1393` · `:1399`) and the headline still cannot be lifted, because item `(4a)`'s census read is charged nothing at all. FROM THIS DATE THE HEADLINE READS: *"the unit may not claim that its render guard is bounded, and may claim the cost unit of `§2.1`'s clause of `2026-10-24` on `F7-2`, item `(4c)`, ONLY FROM THE DATE ARM `(xvii)`'S BYTE LANDS."*** **(5b) HEADLINE `2`, QUOTED AND WITHDRAWN — IT IS FALSE AT THE SHIPPED BYTE: `F6-1`'s clause of `2026-10-24` item `(1c)` reads *"EVERY READ THE RENDER GUARD PERFORMS IS CHARGED TO THE SAME ONE PER-CALL BUDGET"*.** **IT IS NOT TRUE: the census reads at `:886`, `:974`, `:840` and `:983`, the reference-set memberships at `:854` and `:891`, and the key iteration at `:1195` are charged NOTHING, and item `(4b)` prints the multiplication that follows. THE SENTENCE FROM THIS DATE READS: *"EVERY READ THE RENDER GUARD PERFORMS IS CHARGED TO THE SAME ONE PER-CALL BUDGET ONCE ARM `(xvii)`'S BYTE LANDS — one position per read, a census read being charged one plus the length of the list it returns, and a link one for its entry — and UNTIL THEN ONLY THE DESCRIPTOR READS, THE ELEMENT INDEX READS AND THE LINK ENTRIES ARE CHARGED."*** **(5c) THE FINDING'S OWN HEADLINE, RE-DERIVED SO A DONE ROW'S QUOTATION IS EXACTLY TRUE: *"the render guard is bounded"* is claimable ONLY in the form of item `(5b)`'s sentence read at its figure — *no more than `1,000,000` read units per call of `renderGuardTrips`, one unit per link entered and per element index read, and one unit per member of every census read plus one for the read, with exhaustion answering "caller code is reachable" so the arm renders its class spelling* — and NEVER as the four-word absolute, which the shipped byte falsifies.**

**(6) THE CLAIM SENTENCE, RE-DERIVED AT ITS OWN BOUNDARY — AND IT GAINS A SECOND SENTENCE BECAUSE THIS PASS'S DEFECT IS NOT A USER-CODE DEFECT.**

**(6a) THE SENTENCE AS FILED, QUOTED EXACTLY — `F6-3`'s clause of `2026-10-24`, item `(3c)`: *no user code the caller supplied as the refused value's conversion path runs on a refusal path — except (i) where that path lies on a prototype judged language-standard by shape at the two conditional arms AND IS AN OVERRIDDEN `toString`, `valueOf`, or — at an array's own `toString` — AN OVERRIDDEN `join`; (ii) at a `(d)(i)` carrier position — a `targetSpelling` slot, which carries no render guard — whose declared descriptor IS the value's own rendering, where that rendering runs, because it is the descriptor, every member it reaches: the value's `@@toPrimitive` (the first lookup `ToPrimitive` makes, a `[[Get]]` that traverses the chain), its `toString` (the first method `OrdinaryToPrimitive` takes at the string hint), its `valueOf` (reached where that `toString` yields a non-primitive), and its `@@toStringTag` (read by `Object.prototype.toString`'s own `[[Get]]`); and (iii) through the further reads the sanctioned rendering itself performs, where an index carried only by a prototype is reached by `join`'s `[[Get]]` although the element limb's own-descriptor read cannot see it.*** **IT IS NOW EXACTLY TRUE AS FILED, AND THE RE-DERIVATION IS THAT NOTHING OF IT MOVES: the `F5-5` clause's item `(5d)` — *"and it is claimable AT FULL LENGTH ONLY"* — STANDS, that item's conditional fourth limb — *"the position rendering is a FOURTH LIMB UNTIL ARM `(x)`'S BYTE LANDS"* — is DISCHARGED, because arm `(x)`'s byte stands at this pass's read (`valuePosition` `:1128`–`:1150` is a local index loop and `spellConstraintAddress`'s sibling defect is row `39`, a DIFFERENT path), and limbs `(i)`, `(ii)` and `(iii)` stand byte-identical.** **(6b) THE SECOND SENTENCE, AND IT IS THIS PASS'S OWN — A CALLER'S ONE-LINER DECIDES A VERDICT, WHICH THE SENTENCE ABOVE DOES NOT REACH AT ALL: *no verdict, class, status, position, subject byte or crossed member is decided by any method, iterator, iteration protocol or coercion taken from a mutable prototype, at any of the crossing's five paths — except the parked override family of limb (i), whose answer IS the subject at the two conditional arms.*** **(6c) THE CLAIMABILITY RULE, AND IT IS THE WHOLE REASON THE SENTENCE IS PRINTED: THE PAIR IS CLAIMABLE AT FULL LENGTH AND ONLY TOGETHER — a DONE row that quotes item `(6a)` without item `(6b)` claims less than this contract now states, and a DONE row that quotes item `(6b)` BEFORE the sweep of item `(10)` lands must append *"and except at the sites `§2.1`'s clause of `2026-10-24` on `F7-1`, item `(2b)`, marks `OWED`, where a caller's one-liner decides the verdict."*** **NO DONE ROW MAY QUOTE LIMB `(i)` ALONE, LIMBS `(i)` AND `(ii)` ALONE, OR ITEM `(5b)`'S SENTENCE WITHOUT ITS FIGURE.**

**(7) THE PARKED RESIDUES (`F7-3`'S LIST): NAMED, OWNED, AND NOT BLOCKERS.**

**(7a) `R1` — THE OVERRIDE FAMILY.** An overridden `toString`, `valueOf` or (at an array's own `toString`) `join` ON A SHAPE-STANDARD PROTOTYPE RUNS at the two conditional arms, because the carrying test asks whether the NAME is own and never whether the member's VALUE is the intrinsic. **OWNER: the pass that re-opens `C-11` — no read this contract allows distinguishes an intrinsic from an override, so closing `R1` needs a pristine identity and `C-11` must be re-opened first (`§2.1`'s clause of `2026-10-24` on `F-RA-22`, items `(b)` and `(d)`, is its home). REVISIT TRIGGER: any pass that takes a pristine `Function.prototype.toString` identity, or the architect's re-opening of `C-11`. NOT A BLOCKER: its size, its owner and its trigger stand as `F-RA-22` filed them, and item `(2b)`'s row `42` is `PARKED` and not `OWED`.** **(7b) `R2` — THE ADMISSION PATH'S CENSUS COST.** The WALK charges NODES, not READS: `protoClass`/`arrayClass`/`isStandardObjectPrototype`/`accessorOrSymbolArm` read a census on every classification and `NODE_CEILING` counts positions, so a value whose prototype carries `10^7` own names costs `10^7` reads for ONE node. **OWNER: the pass that may re-derive `§8` ROW `8`'s MEANING cell, because charging census reads on the walk would change what the register's integer counts and a register row is outside this clause. REVISIT TRIGGER: the first `write` measurement whose census cost exceeds its node count by more than an order of magnitude, or any pass that touches `§8` row `8`. NOT A BLOCKER: item `(4c)`'s unit is the GUARD's, and this residue is the walk's, stated here so no DONE row claims the admission path's cost bounded by it.** **(7c) `R3` — THE SANCTIONED RENDERING'S FURTHER READS BEYOND THE PARKED FAMILY, AND THE `(d)(i)` CARRIER RENDERING'S.** Limb `(iii)` of item `(6a)` concedes the reads the sanctioned rendering performs, and limb `(ii)` concedes the four members a `(d)(i)` slot reaches; **the `(d)(i)` slots' `targetSpelling` (`:1042`) carries no render guard at all** (`F-RA-18`'s clause of `2026-10-24`, item `(1)(b)`), **so the value's own rendering runs there and every member it reaches runs with it.** **OWNER: `R1`'s pass (the same identity question, at two more sites). REVISIT TRIGGER: `R1`'s. NOT A BLOCKER: both are LETTERS of a declared law under `surface.md §2.5` item 5 (a), and no byte of this pass moves either.** **(7d) THE RULE THAT MAKES THE LIST A LIST AND NOT A DEFECT LOG: a `PARKED` residue is NOT a blocker, NOT an owed byte and NOT a cell — it is a declared state whose owner and trigger are named — and NO PASS MAY RE-OPEN ONE UNDER THIS CLAUSE; a pass that means to close one says so under its own clause and names the item it supersedes.**

**(8) NO CELL MAY ASSERT, AND WHAT DOES NOT MOVE.**

**(8a) NO CELL MAY ASSERT A COUNT OF READS, OF LINK VISITS OR OF CENSUS MEMBERS — the bound is the law and not an instrumented figure — and no cell may assert a verdict, class or subject DERIVED FROM A FORCED PROTOTYPE, because that is the state the law forbids; the battery cell of item `(9)` asserts exactly the negative of each.** **(8b) WHAT DOES NOT MOVE: the seventeen `§2` rows and their class spellings and positions, the status and rank, PART ONE's and PART TWO's bytes, `§8`'s `8` register rows (rows `2`, `3` and `6` byte-identical), `§6`'s residue rows and owners, the two ceilings and their figures, `§4`'s copy discipline, `§5`'s copy-out form, the six gates' run figures printed elsewhere in this contract, and every `ALREADY LAWFUL` row of item `(2b)` — which is to say `F5-4`'s and `F6-2`'s bytes stand and are NOT re-opened by this pass.** **(8c) AND THE ONE THING THIS CLAUSE ADDS TO A CELL'S OBLIGATION IS NEGATIVE: a cell may now fail a build that answers a verdict the caller decided, which no landed cell could see — `test-cases.md`'s clause of this date mints the battery that drives it.**

**(10) THE CODE-OWED LEDGER, IN DEPENDENCY ORDER — FIVE ARMS (`xiii`–`xvii`) IN `implementation-plan.md §2` STEP `4`'S CLAUSE OF THIS DATE.**

**(10a) `(xiii)` — `S2` + `S3` + `S4`, THE CENSUS MACHINERY MADE MEMBER-FREE (`15` sites: rows `1`–`14` and `12`'s two members).** **DEPENDS ON NOTHING, and NOTHING OF `(xiv)` DEPENDS ON ITS ANSWER — but it goes FIRST because every later arm's charge accounting is taken over the reads it makes lawful.** **(10b) `(xiv)` — `S1`, `S7`, EVERY ARRAY ITERATION AND SPREAD AND BOTH `includes` SITES (`11 + 3` sites minus `54`'s ownership split: rows `5` · `15` · `18` · `19` · `20` · `21` · `23` · `25` · `29` · `31` · `33` · `44` · `46`, and `54` if its owner does not claim it).** **DEPENDS ON NOTHING; it is the arm a red cell cannot see today precisely because the battery is the FIRST cell that drives it.** **(10c) `(xv)` — `S5` + `S6` + `S8`, THE ALLOWANCE'S MEMO, `push`, AND THE BIGINT'S OWN RENDERING (`2 + 5 + 1` sites: rows `30` · `32` · `22` · `24` · `43` · `45` · `47` · `26`).** **DEPENDS ON NOTHING; it is LAST OF THE SWEEP because it touches no admission and no verdict, and it is still OWED because a caller's override there writes the package's own refusal datum.** **(10e) `(xvii)` — THE COST UNIT OF ITEM `(4c)`, THE CHARGE FOR EVERY CENSUS READ (`:886` · `:974` · `:840` · `:983` and the reference-set memberships at `:854`/`:891`).** **DEPENDS ON `(xiii)` AND `(xv)` AND NOTHING ELSE, and NOTHING MAY LAND BEFORE IT AND CLAIM IT: the reads it charges are only well-defined once no census read is a prototype read (`(xiii)`) and once the memo's charge path is the unit's (`(xv)`).** **(10f) THE ORDER BETWEEN THE FIVE IS THE ORDER PRINTED, AND IT IS A DEPENDENCY ORDER AND NOT A PREFERENCE: `(xiii)` → `(xiv)` → `(xv)` → `(xvi)` → `(xvii)`, with `(xiv)` and `(xvi)` independent of `(xiii)` and landing wherever the implementer's pass places them, and `(xvii)` LAST BY DEPENDENCE. EACH RED COMES FIRST AND EACH BYTE SECOND, and the unit may not claim `F7-1`'s sweep, `F7-2`'s unit or `F7-3`'s residues closed before the arm that closes it lands.**

**⟶ AMENDED 2026-10-24 — THE GATE-11 EIGHTH AUDIT PASS (read-only, `2026-10-24`; findings `F8-1` · `F8-2` · `F8-3` · `F8-4` · `F8-5` · `F8-6` · `F8-7`; `RCA-8(g)`): THE INVENTORY IS COMPLETED AT THE ONE PLACE IT WAS NOT — THE RECORD-COPY AND DELIVERY FAMILY AND THE ARGUMENT/CONSTRUCTION FAMILY ARE ENUMERATED BY SYMBOL, LINE, ARM AND CELL; THE GOVERNED PATHS MOVE `5 → 6`; THE COST UNIT CHARGES THE TWO CALLER-SCALED IDENTITY LOOPS IT CHARGED NOTHING; ITEM `(10a)`'S REMEDY IS RE-DERIVED BECAUSE A SYMBOL PRIMITIVE OWNS NO `description`; ITEM `(10e)`'S CLAIM IS RE-DERIVED TO THE INERT STATE IT IS IN; AND TWO CODE-BYTES ARE RECORDED AS OWED TO THE PASSES THAT OWN THEM.**

**The clause of this date on `F7-1`, `F7-2` and `F7-3` above — its law, its positive form, its boundary, its grounds, its inventory's rows `1`–`54`, its census, its cost unit, its residues, its cells and its five arms `(xiii)`–`(xvii)` — stands byte-for-byte and is read THROUGH this one, EXCEPT the marks this clause re-derives AT THEIR OWN ITEMS AND NOWHERE ELSE: item `(1g)`'s FIVE PATHS MOVE TO SIX — the sixth path declared and bounded at item `(2c)` below — item `(2b)`'s TABLE GAINS ROWS `55`–`65` IN PLACE (a site is written where the inventory is written, and an appended table row moves no existing row's bytes), item `(2c)`'s CENSUS IS RE-FIGURED at item `(8b)` below, item `(4c)`'s `EXHAUSTIVE` ENUMERATION IS RE-DERIVED at item `(3c)` below, item `(10a)` IS RE-DERIVED at item `(5)` below, and item `(10e)` IS RE-DERIVED at item `(7)` below.** **NO CLASS, NO STATUS, NO POSITION, NO MESSAGE BYTE, NO `§2` ROW, NO `§8` REGISTER ROW — `§8`'s rows `2`, `3` and `6` STAND BYTE-IDENTICAL — NO RUN FIGURE AND NO ADMISSION IS RE-DECIDED HERE, and the status line above stands byte-for-byte because this clause changes no gate status.** **EVERY CODE FIGURE THIS CLAUSE PRINTS IS MEASURED AT THIS PASS'S READ, WHICH IS ONE LANDING LATER THAN THE `F7` CLAUSE'S READ, SO EACH SUPERSEDES THE EARLIER FIGURE FOR THE SAME SYMBOL — `renderGuardTrips` `:1711` (printed `:1441`), `spendGuardRead` `:1685`, `arrayElementsReachCallerCode` `:1602` (printed `:1373`), `carriesConversionMember` `:1456` (printed `:1250`), `encodeValue` `:1972` (printed `:1698`), `arrayClass` `:1108` (printed `:960`), `isStandardObjectPrototype` `:986` (printed `:872`) and `plainProtoNames` `:874` (printed `:814`) being the eight the `F7` clause names itself.**

**(1) `F8-1` — THE RECORD-COPY AND DELIVERY FAMILY IS ENUMERATED AS ROWS `55`–`60`, AND THE TWO MEASURED CONSEQUENCES MAKE ITS ABSENCE A BLOCKER AND NOT A GAP OF TIDINESS.**

**(1a) THE SITES AT THEIR BYTES, MEASURED AT THIS PASS'S READ. `crossTriggered` (`:3075`) copies the reply's trigger set with `for (const element of triggered)` (`:3078`) and `out.push({ handle: element.handle, address: decodeSegments(element.address) })` (`:3079`); `handlesOf` (`:3086`) reads the set's handles with `triggered.map((element) => element.handle)` (`:3087`); the `write` member (`:3470`) builds the record's watch set with `watched.push(decodeSegments(answer.watched[i]))` (`:3576`), inside an index loop that item `(1c)(i)` already declares; and `deliver` (`:4462`) dedupes with `new Set()` (`:4499`), `Set.prototype.has` (`:4502`) and `Set.prototype.add` (`:4503`) inside `for (const item of triggered)` (`:4501`), appends with `recipients.push([item.handle, …])` (`:4510`), and invokes through `for (const [handle, listener] of recipients)` (`:4512`), whose pair destructuring consumes each pair array through `Array.prototype[Symbol.iterator]`. THE ALLOCATION `new Set()` IS LAWFUL BY ITEM `(1d)(iv)` AND IS NOT OWED; ITS INSTANCE'S TWO METHODS AND THE ITERATIONS ARE, exactly as that item declares.**

**(1b) THE TWO MEASURED CONSEQUENCES, EACH IN A FORM `(1e)` FORBIDS. (i) A forged `Array.prototype[Symbol.iterator]` that yields one `undefined` makes `element.handle` at `:3079` throw a RAW HOST `TypeError` out of a `write` the walk ADMITTED AND THE STORE ALREADY WROTE — the commit is durable, and the failure carries no class, no status and no position: the one form `docs/specs/storeroom-js/surface.md §2.5` item 5 (e) and `A76` forbid, and the direction item `(1e)(iii)` names. (ii) An `Array.prototype.push` that answers `0` and copies nothing silently empties the record at both sites — `triggered` (`:3079`) and `watched` (`:3576`) — so the record crosses EMPTY and `deliver` finds no recipient: THE LISTENER DOES NOT FIRE, nothing is refused, and no byte of the package says so. That is item `(1e)(i)`'s ground at its strongest form, because a caller's one-liner has DECIDED A DELIVERY without deciding a verdict — and a delivery is what a listener-bearing construction exists for.**

**(1c) WHY THE FAMILY WAS ABSENT, STATED SO THE GAP IS NOT READ AS A SECOND LAW. The law's operative sentence (item `(1b)`) governs the crossing's COPY path, and item `(1g)(v)` enumerated that path as `encodeValue`'s member construction, `decodeValue` and `decodeSegments` — the VALUE TREE's copy. `decodeSegments` therefore stood in the inventory (row `47`) while `crossTriggered`, WHICH CALLS IT AT `:3079`, did not: the reply's copy and the delivery stood outside a list whose own header sentence is *"EVERY REMAINING SITE … a site absent from it is a site the law will find later"*. THE LAW IS UNMOVED AND ITS PATH IS WIDENED AT `(1g)(v)`, WHICH IS THE ONLY THING THIS FINDING RE-DERIVES BESIDE THE ROWS.**

**(1d) THE MARKINGS, TWO UNITS AND ONE CELL, AS THE ROWS PRINT THEM: rows `55`, `56`, `57`, `58`, `59` and `60` are `OWED`; row `55` and row `60` join `S1`, row `58` joins `S3`, rows `57` and `59` join `S6`, and row `56` joins the unit this pass mints, `S10` (item `(2c)`); all six are arm `(xviii)`'s and all six are driven by `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-64`, WHICH IS THE UNIT'S FIRST LISTENER-BEARING CELL — the landed set drives no delivery at all, so no cell of it reaches this family.**

**(2) `F8-2` — THE ARGUMENT AND CONSTRUCTION FAMILY IS ENUMERATED AS ROWS `61`–`64`, AND THE CHOICE MADE HERE IS `OWED` — FOR THIS UNIT — WITH ITS FOUR GROUNDS.**

**(2a) THE SITES AT THEIR BYTES: `encodeTier` (`:2590`) builds the tier set with `tier.map(encodeTierSetItem)` (`:2646`); `encodeListeners` (`:2761`) walks the list with `listeners.map(…)` (`:2767`); `encodeConstraints` (`:2795`) walks the fourth key with `constraints.map(…)` (`:2801`); and `storeCreate` (`:3347`) seeds the construction's own registration with `new Map()` (`:4349`), `Map.prototype.get` (`:4352`), `Map.prototype.set` (`:4353`), `set.push(entry.address)` (`:4354`), `for (const entry of cfg.listeners)` (`:4350`) and `for (const [handle, set] of byHandle)` (`:4356`). THE THREE ALLOCATIONS ARE LAWFUL BY ITEM `(1d)(iv)`; THE MEMBERS AND THE ITERATIONS ARE NOT.**

**(2b) THE MEASURED CONSEQUENCES: `delete Array.prototype.map` makes `storeCreate({ journalLength: 8, listeners: [] })` throw a RAW HOST `TypeError` — out of a CONSTRUCTION, before it returns, with no class and no status — which is `F-RA-3`'s item `(3)` direction inverted and the same direction row `57`'s sibling byte was landed to close at `:3576`; and a skipping `Symbol.iterator` at `:4350` makes the construction's DECLARED SEEDING silently register nothing while `storeCreate` returns an instance whose mirror is empty. A forged `map` at `:2646` silently drops tier items from a verb that then succeeds. EACH OF THE THREE IS A CROSSED MEMBER OR A RAW HOST ERROR DECIDED BY A PROTOTYPE THE CALLER OWNS.**

**(2c) THE CHOICE, STATED IN THE FORM THE FINDING ASKS FOR: `OWED` FOR THIS UNIT — NOT `ROUTED` — AND THE GROUND IS FOURFOLD. (i) THE FORBIDDEN DIRECTION IS THE MEASURED ONE AND IT IS THIS UNIT'S OWN: a raw host error out of a lawful construction is exactly what `§2`'s clause of this date on `F-RA-3`, item `(3)`, declares inadmissible for a DELETION in the caller's realm, and this unit has already owed and landed the identical byte one screen away (row `57`, `:3576` under the same finding family) — a class swept in half a file and called swept is the false record `F8-4` names. (ii) `§1`'s subject caption WITHHOLDS CARRIER ADDRESSES, TIERS, OP-IDS AND HANDLES AS SUBJECTS — their domains, rows and spellings — and IT DOES NOT WITHHOLD THE MECHANISM BY WHICH THE CROSSING READS A CALLER'S OWN ARRAY; this finding re-decides NO row, NO spelling and NO domain of `TierArg`, of `ListenersArg` or of the `constraints` key. (iii) NO OTHER UNIT OF RECORD OWNS THIS FILE: the routing rows `49`–`53` go to units that own ADDRESS data and the registry, and no unit anywhere is named the owner of `bindings/js/index.mjs`'s construction encoders — so a `ROUTED` row would name an owner that does not exist, which the marking enum's own definition (`ROUTED` = *"another unit's or another pass's subject"*, item `(2a)`) forbids: THE PASS THAT WILL NEXT TOUCH THIS FILE IS THIS UNIT'S OWN. (iv) A `ROUTED` ROW WOULD LET A DONE ROW CLAIM THE LAW WHILE A MEASURED STATE STANDS, which item `(8a)`'s negative and item `(1e)(iii)`'s fail-closed direction exist to prevent.** **AND THE WIDENING IS DECLARED AND BOUNDED: the sixth path is `storeCreate`'s ARGUMENT AND CONSTRUCTION ENCODERS — `encodeTier`, `encodeListeners`, `encodeConstraints`, `constraintEntry` (`:2808`) and `encodeTierSetItem` (`:2654`), and `storeCreate`'s own construction block (`:4349`–`:4358`) — AND THE CARRIER ADDRESS ENCODERS ARE NOT IN IT: `encodeAddress`, `constraintAddress` and `inDomainAddress` (`:4584`) are reached FROM those encoders and remain rows `49`/`50`'s routing under item `(3)`.**

**(2d) THE MARKINGS, TWO UNITS AND ONE CELL: rows `61`, `62`, `63` and `64` are `OWED`; rows `61`, `62` and `63` join `S10` and row `64` joins `S5`; all four are arm `(xix)`'s and all four are driven by `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-65`.**

**(3) `F8-3` — THE COST UNIT CHARGES TWO CALLER-SCALED IDENTITY LOOPS AT ZERO, AND ITEM `(4c)`'S `EXHAUSTIVE` ENUMERATION IS RE-DERIVED HERE.**

**(3a) THE STATE, AT ITS BYTES AND MEASURED AT THIS PASS'S READ. `spendGuardRead` (`:1685`) exists and its doc block (`:1674`–`:1684`) declares the unit `EXHAUSTIVE` — *"an allocation, a `typeof`, an identity compare and a spelled relational comparison are `0`"* (`:1679`). BUT THE GUARD RUNS TWO CALLER-SCALED IDENTITY LOOPS CHARGED NOTHING: the CYCLE TEST over `path` (`:1610`–`:1615`, a spelled `===` per path entry), and the MEMO CONSULTATION (`:1622`–`:1627`, a spelled `===` per recorded entry — the memo the sweep installed as a PAIR LIST at `:1716` and grew at `:1644`). THE PATH IS COPIED BY A THIRD UNCARGED LOOP (`extendedPath`, `:1653`, its copy at `:1655`). Only the memo HIT charges, and it charges the repeat's RECORDED POSITIONS (`:1629`) — never the scan that found it.**

**(3b) THE WITNESS AND THE ARITHMETIC, PRINTED SO THE FINDING IS CHECKABLE. Drive `~125,000` DISTINCT leaves — each `Object.setPrototypeOf([], null)`, so each is a fresh identity with a null prototype — as the elements of one array refused at row `8`'s DEPTH arm: every element passes the path test, spends its own position and consults a memo that has grown by one entry per element, so the identity comparisons performed inside ONE `1,000,000`-unit call are `1 + 2 + … + 125,000 ≈ 125,000²/2 ≈ 7.8×10⁹`, and every one of them is charged `0`. THE BUDGET ADMITS `~125,000` MEMO ENTRIES BECAUSE THE LIST IT GROWS IS NEVER CHARGED: the guard is bounded in POSITIONS and unbounded in READS, which is the same defect item `(4a)` fixed for the census read and left standing for these two loops. WITH THE RE-DERIVED UNIT THE SAME DRIVE EXHAUSTS IMMEDIATELY: the consultation charges `1 + memo.length`, so the cumulative charge after `k` elements is `k²/2 + c·k` and the budget of `1,000,000` is spent near the `1,400`th distinct leaf — the arm then answers its class spelling without performing the scan, WITH THE WALL-CLOCK CEILING `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-67` pins. THIS ARITHMETIC LIVES HERE AND NOT IN THE CELL: item `(8a)` forbids a cell to assert a read count.**

**(3c) ITEM `(4c)`'S ENUMERATION, RE-DERIVED AND STILL `EXHAUSTIVE` — ELEVEN TERMS, EACH WITH THE SITE THAT TAKES IT, SUPERSEDING THE NINE THE DOC BLOCK PRINTS: (i) one `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor` call is `1` (`:1470` · `:1519` · `:1527` · `:1639`); (ii) one `Object.getPrototypeOf` call is `1` (`:999` · `:1408` · `:1461` · `:1481` · `:1521` · `:1529`); (iii) an own-NAME census read is `1 + the length of the list it returns` (`:876` · `:890` · `:1012` · `:1144`); (iv) an own-SYMBOL census read is `1 + the length of the list it returns` (`:927` · `:1145`); (v) an element index read is `1`, charged where it is taken through `spendGuardPosition` (`:1618` · `:1638`); (vi) a link entered is `1`, charged by the chain loops' own `getPrototypeOf` spends (`:1461` · `:1481` · `:1521` · `:1529`, beside `:1408`'s one-link form), the spender itself being `spendGuardPosition` (`:1665`); (vii) a memo HIT is the repeat's recorded count (`:1629`); **(viii) A MEMO CONSULTATION IS `1 + memo.length`** (`:1622`–`:1627`) — **NEW**; **(ix) THE PATH IS `1` PER ENTRY IT CARRIES, CHARGED AT BOTH LIMBS — THE TEST'S SCAN (`:1610`–`:1615`) AND THE COPY (`:1655`)** — **NEW, and the scan is charged because it is the loop the finding measures: charging only the copy would leave the caller-scaled test at `0`**; (x) an allocation and a `typeof` are `0` (`:1716` · `:1603`); and (xi) **AN IDENTITY COMPARE AND A SPELLED RELATIONAL COMPARISON ARE `0` ONLY WHERE THEY ARE NOT ONE OF TERMS `(viii)` AND `(ix)`** — the doc block's sentence at `:1679` is re-derived to exactly that, and **THAT BYTE IS OWED WITH ARM `(xvii)` AND NOT BEFORE IT.** **TERMS `(i)`–`(vii)` AND `(x)` STAND AS THE ARM LANDED THEM; `NO SECOND FIGURE IS MINTED` — `NODE_CEILING` (`1,000,000`, `:1916`) stays the one per-call budget, created once per `renderGuardTrips` call (`:1716`) — and EXHAUSTION IS NOT A TRIP DECISION (item `(1d)`), which this re-derivation does not touch.**

**(3d) THE CODE OWED, AND ITS CELL: the two limbs are charged through `spendGuardRead` at their own sites — the memo consultation `1 + memo.length` before its scan, the path `1` per entry at the test and at the copy, each answering "caller code is reachable" where the spend is refused, exactly as the limb's other spends do (`:1618` · `:1629` · `:1638`) — and the doc byte at `:1679` is corrected with them. `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-67` is the cell: `~125,000` distinct null-prototype leaves at row `8`'s DEPTH arm, the class spelling at the depth arm's own position INSIDE A WALL-CLOCK CEILING, with the PRIMITIVE-LEAF control that must still complete and render its join — so the cell proves the cost was the memo and not the fan-out. ARM `(xvii)` IS THE ARM THAT CARRIES IT (item `(10e)` re-derived, and `implementation-plan.md §2` step `4`'s clause of this date).**

**(4) `F8-4` — THE PLAN'S SCOPE ROW IS STALE AND ONE EDIT STANDS IN ANOTHER UNIT'S FILE: THE RULING IS REVERT-AND-ROUTE, AND ROW `(g)` IS SATISFIED WITHOUT THE KERNEL EDIT.**

**(4a) THE TWO DEFECTS, MEASURED. (i) `bindings/js/src/constraint-kernel.mjs`'s `append` (`:382`) was edited at `:389` — `attempt.log.push({…})` became `attempt.log[attempt.log.length] = {…}` — with a licence comment at `:383`–`:388` that names this contract's `F7-1` as its authority, and with NO scope row (`implementation-plan.md §3` names `bindings/js/src/constraint-kernel.mjs` NOWHERE), NO amendment row in the plan and NO inventory row in item `(2b)`. (ii) Plan item `(4)`'s sentence *"every one of which is a function that row `4` already names, so NO SCOPE ROW IS WIDENED"* IS FALSE AT THE LANDING: row `4`'s cell names `encodeValue`, `boundaryReject`/`valueReject` and `decodeValue` ONLY, and the sweep edited the census readers, both renderers, the argument encoders, the verbs' argument lists, the attempt seam and the record and delivery functions — and one file no row names at all. A SCOPE ROW THAT IS READ AS UNWIDENED WHILE FIVE FAMILIES AND A FOREIGN FILE MOVED IS A LICENCE DEFECT, NOT A CITATION DEFECT.**

**(4b) THE RULING — REVERT AND ROUTE — ON THREE GROUNDS, EACH RESTATED AT ITS OWN BYTES. (i) THAT FILE IS THE CONSTRAINTS UNIT'S DECLARED HOME: `docs/specs/storeroom-constraints/kernel-surface.md §1` item 1 declares *"the kernel module's ONE checkout home is the ESM SOURCE FILE `bindings/js/src/constraint-kernel.mjs`"*, so a byte there is that unit's subject and cannot be licensed by this unit's finding. (ii) ITS OTHER SITES OF THE SAME CLASS ARE UNSWEPT — `attempt.ran.push` (`:835`), `attempt.ops.push` (`:860` · `:1667`), `attempt.appliedRepairs.push` (`:861` · `:1668`), `callList.push` (`:1965`), `attempt.rollback.push` (`:2022`), `performed.push` (`:2035`), `recorded.push` (`:2039`), `out.push` (`:1400`), the log's own `indexOf`/`splice` (`:401` · `:403`), `attempt.ran.includes` (`:1520`), `attempt.appliedRepairs.includes` (`:912` · `:1530` · `:1544`), `KC_TOKENS.includes` (`:1626`), the two `.map(...).join('/')` spellings (`:263` · `:945`), the `for…of` over `kernel.pairs` (`:274` · `:282` · `:297` · `:1397`) and over `LIVE.values()` (`:1419`), and `byId = new Map()` (`:242`) — the audit's `~57` same-class sites, of which the sweep touched ONE. ONE SWEPT LINE BESIDE FIFTY-SEVEN UNSWEPT ONES READS AS A SWEPT KERNEL, AND THAT READING IS FALSE: the comment at `:383`–`:388` is the only byte that says otherwise. (iii) An edit that lands in another unit's file under this unit's finding is a licence defect whatever its bytes are worth — and the bytes' worth is real, which is exactly why the constraints unit must land them under its own clause.**

**(4c) WHAT THE REVERT IS, EXACTLY: `:389`'s index write goes back to the shipped `attempt.log.push({ seq: attempt.log.length + 1, kind, id: …, opId: …, message, state: … })`, and the licence comment `:383`–`:388` goes with it, so that file carries NO byte of this unit's sweep and no reader takes a swept kernel from one line. THE REVERT IS AN IMPLEMENTER'S ACT (this pass writes documents and no code), IT IS OWED BEFORE ANY CLAIM THAT THIS UNIT'S SWEEP IS COMPLETE, and it moves NO behaviour: `attempt.log.push` and the index write are the same append with the same `seq` on every tree whose `Array.prototype.push` is the intrinsic.**

**(4d) ROW `(g)` IS SATISFIED WITHOUT THE KERNEL EDIT, STATED PLAINLY BECAUSE THE FINDING ASKS FOR THE PLAIN ANSWER. The battery's member `(g)` — `Array.prototype.push` answering `0` without copying — is driven at THE CROSSING'S OWN `push` SITES, which are rows `22`, `24`, `43`, `45`, `47` and now `55`, `57`, `59` and `64`: the crossed value's members at `:1851` · `:1887`, the copy-out's at `:2593` · `:2605` · `:2633`, and the record's and the construction's at `:3079` · `:3576` · `:4510` · `:4354`. **SO THE CELL MUST ASSERT THE CROSSED VALUE'S MEMBERS AND THE RECORD'S `triggered` AND `watched` — the admitted tree and the delivery — AND MUST NOT ASSERT THE ATTEMPT LOG AT ALL.** **AND THE ONE THING THAT CANNOT BE SATISFIED WITHOUT A KERNEL EDIT IS SAID HERE RATHER THAN LEFT TO A LATER READER: a cell asserting that the ATTEMPT LOG survives a no-op `push` CANNOT BE WRITTEN FROM THIS UNIT, because it would need the very edit this ruling reverts. **⟶ AMENDED 2026-10-24 — THE COUNTER-NARROWING PASS (`2026-10-24`; the implementer's measured red set, taken AFTER arms `(xvii)`, `(xviii)` and `(xix)` landed and the kernel byte was reverted as item `(4c)` ruled; finding `F8-8`; `RCA-8(g)`): THE BATTERY'S COUNTER IS NARROWED TO THE CROSSING'S OWN GOVERNED SITES — EXPLICITLY EXCLUDING EVERY TAKE MADE INSIDE `bindings/js/src/constraint-kernel.mjs` — AND THE KERNEL'S CLASS IS ENTERED AS INVENTORY ROW `66`, `ROUTED` TO `U-STOREROOM-CONSTRAINTS` WITH A NAMED OWNER AND A REVISIT CONDITION, BECAUSE FOUR CELLS ARE RED FOR THEIR COUNTERS ALONE AND THE TAKES THAT KEEP THEM RED ARE ANOTHER UNIT'S.**

**The clause of this date on `F7-1`, `F7-2` and `F7-3` above and the clause of this date on the eighth audit above — their law, their positive form, their boundary, their grounds, the inventory's rows `1`–`65`, its markings, its cost unit, its residues, its cells and every arm `(xiii)`–`(xix)` — stand byte-for-byte and are read THROUGH this one, EXCEPT the marks this clause re-derives AT THEIR OWN ITEMS AND NOWHERE ELSE: item `(2)`'s HEADER SENTENCE is re-derived at item `(4)` below; item `(2b)`'s TABLE GAINS ROW `66` IN PLACE (a site is written where the inventory is written, and an appended table row moves no existing row's bytes); item `(2c)`'s and item `(8b)`'s CENSUSES ARE RE-FIGURED at item `(5)`; item `(10)`'s LEDGER GAINS ITS SCOPE ITEM `(10g)` at item `(6)`; and the four counter-bearing cells are re-derived at item `(7)`. NO CLASS, NO STATUS, NO POSITION, NO MESSAGE BYTE, NO `§2` ROW, NO `§8` REGISTER ROW — rows `2`, `3` and `6` STAND BYTE-IDENTICAL — NO ADMISSION, NO MARKING, AND NO FIGURE OF ANY GATE RUN IS RE-DECIDED HERE; the status line above stands byte-for-byte because this clause changes no gate status, and NO CELL IS MINTED, the declared case set therefore staying `67`.**

**(1) THE MEASURED STATE, AND WHY IT FORCES A NARROWING RATHER THAN A SIXTH SWEEP.**

**(1a) THE THREE ARMS STAND AT THE SHIPPED BYTES, READ AT THIS PASS'S READ.** `crossTriggered` (`:3145`) copies the trigger set by item `(1c)(i)`'s index loop and appends by `out[out.length] = …` (`:3148`–`:3150`); `handlesOf` (`:3162`) transforms by index (`:3164`); the `write` member's watch set is an index write; and `deliver` (`:4583`) dedupes over a local handle list with item `(1c)(vi)`'s spelled `===` (`:4632`–`:4638`), appends by index and reads each pair by index. `encodeTier`, `encodeListeners` (`:2799`, its own loop declared at `:2805`–`:2808`) and `encodeConstraints` walk by index, and `storeCreate`'s seeding is a local pair list read by index (`:4458`–`:4474`) with `instance.listen` called once per handle off it (`:4477`–`:4479`) — **NO `Map.prototype.get`/`set`, no `set.push`, no `for…of` and no pair destructuring.** Arm `(xvii)`'s two charges stand too: the path is charged `1` per entry at both limbs (`:1615` · `:1653`) and the memo consultation `1 + memo.length` before its scan (`:1636`–`:1638`). **AND A LANDING IS NOT A GREEN: no cell's pass is claimed by this item, which reads bytes.**

**(1b) FOUR CELLS ARE RED FOR THEIR COUNTERS ALONE, AND FOR NOTHING ELSE.** `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-63` at its row `(g)`, `JC-TC-64` at its states `(i)` and `(ii)`, `JC-TC-65` at its states `(ii)` and `(iii)`, and `JC-TC-66`: each asserts `0` on an override's counter, and each is measured red at the counters while its crossed member, its record, its trees and its construction answers hold.

**(1c) THE TAKES THAT KEEP THEM RED ARE THE KERNEL'S, AND THEY ARE MEASURED AT FOUR LINES: `bindings/js/src/constraint-kernel.mjs`'s `append`'s `attempt.log.push` (`:383`) — ONE PER ADMITTED WRITE — and `inDomainAddress`'s `for (const segment of address)` (`:177`), `evaluate`'s `for (const key of list)` (`:1460`) and `releasePending`'s `for (const key of keys)` (`:1729`) — ONE EACH PER ADMITTED WRITE, because every admitted `write` opens an attempt and the kernel writes and reads that attempt's own lists.** **THAT FILE IS UNCONDITIONALLY IN THE CALL: a `write` the walk admitted reaches `constraintWrite`, which opens the kernel's attempt before the store leg — so a counter armed over the whole process necessarily sees these four takes on every admitted write, and NO BYTE THIS UNIT MAY WRITE CAN REMOVE THEM.**

**(1d) THE CELLS AS FILED COUNT "A MEMBER TAKEN ANYWHERE IN THE CALL", AND THAT IS THE DEFECT THIS CLAUSE CLOSES.** A `0` over the whole process is a property of the PROCESS — of every library the call reaches, including another unit's — and it cannot be greened without editing `bindings/js/src/constraint-kernel.mjs`, which item `(4b)` routes and item `(4c)` reverts. **SO THE COUNTER IS NARROWED TO THE CROSSING'S OWN GOVERNED SITES (item `(2)`) AND THE KERNEL'S CLASS IS ROUTED WITH A NAMED OWNER AND A REVISIT CONDITION (item `(5)`) — the other branch of the `F8-4` ruling, and not a reversal of it: NO KERNEL BYTE IS OWED, LICENSED OR CLAIMED BY THIS CLAUSE.**

**(2) THE NARROWED COUNTER LAW.**

**(2a) THE OPERATIVE SENTENCE, AND IT IS THE WHOLE OF THE CHANGE: *a cell's counter asserts `0` takes AT THE CROSSING'S OWN GOVERNED SITES — the sites of the inventory's `OWED`, `ALREADY LAWFUL` and `PARKED` rows — and it asserts NOTHING about the rest of the call: a take attributed to a `ROUTED` row is EXCLUDED from the counter and is that row's own state.***

**(2b) THE GOVERNED SET, NAMED SO NO READER PHRASES IT AS "ANYWHERE": the crossing's own CENSUS READERS, WALK, GUARD, POSITION RENDERERS, COPY PATH, THE RECORD COPY AND THE DELIVERY, the VERBS' ARGUMENT LISTS, the ATTEMPT SEAM, and the ARGUMENT AND CONSTRUCTION ENCODERS with `storeCreate`'s own seeding walk — every one of them a site of a row marked `OWED`, `ALREADY LAWFUL` or `PARKED` in item `(2b)`'s table, and every one of them a function one of the arms `(xiii)`–`(xix)` sweeps (item `(10)`, and the plan's own edit families `1`–`9`).**

**(2c) THE EXCLUDED SET, ENUMERATED BY ROW: EVERY TAKE ATTRIBUTED TO A `ROUTED` ROW, WHICH IS ROW `66` — `bindings/js/src/constraint-kernel.mjs`'s four lines of item `(1c)`, THE EXCLUSION THIS PASS WAS RULED TO MAKE — AND, BY THE SAME RULE APPLIED TO THE SAME MARKING AND NOT AS A SECOND RULE, the `ROUTED` rows whose sites live in the crossing's own file: rows `49` and `50` (`inDomainAddress`'s, `encodeAddress`'s and `constraintAddress`'s `i in address`), `51` (`absentSubject`), `52` (`notFoundRendering`), `53` (`homeAddressKey`, `addressSetKey` and the registry keys) and `65` (the mirror and registry maintenance family).** **THE KERNEL'S EXCLUSION IS THE RULED ONE; the six in-file exclusions are that same marking read the same way, and they cost the four cells nothing at this pass's read, MEASURED: `carriesConstraintAt` answers `false` at `:5547` BEFORE any address key is spelled, so a write on an instance carrying no constraint never reaches rows `53`/`65`'s sites; and the constraint construction's element carries no `constraint` function, so `carryConstraint` is not reached either (`:4374`). A NON-EMPTY `cfg.listeners` list DOES reach row `65` — `storeCreate` applies each handle through `instance.listen` at `:4478` — which is item `(7c)`'s re-derivation.** **AND THE EXCLUSION IS NOT A LOOPHOLE: a take in this file that NO row names is not silently routed — item `(2d)` reports it, and item `(4)` makes it the header's own failure.**

**(2d) THE CLASSIFICATION, DECLARED SO THE TEST WRITER TAKES ONE MECHANISM AND INFERS NONE. Each override wrapper records, at the instant it is invoked, its IMMEDIATE INVOKER — the frame outside the wrapper — and answers that frame's SOURCE FILE and its ENCLOSING SYMBOL; then: (i) file `bindings/js/src/constraint-kernel.mjs` → row `66`, EXCLUDED and counted on the cell's own routed counter, which is RECORDED and never asserted as `0`; (ii) file `bindings/js/index.mjs` and an enclosing symbol that is a `ROUTED` row's site (rows `49`–`53` and `65`) → that row, EXCLUDED on the same routed counter; (iii) file `bindings/js/index.mjs` and an enclosing symbol on a GOVERNED row → GOVERNED, COUNTED, and the cell's assertion is that this count is `0`; (iv) ANY OTHER ANSWER — another file, or no frame that can be read — → REPORTED in the cell's own record and EXCLUDED, never folded into the `0` and never silently dropped, because a take this law cannot attribute is a widening of the routed set that must be VISIBLE.** **THE MECHANISM IS A STACK READ AND NOTHING ELSE: the wrapper's own frame is skipped by construction, the next frame is the answer, and no wrapper may classify by an argument it was handed.**

**(2e) THE POSITIVE FORM, WHICH IS THE WHOLE OF THE ASSERTION AND NOT A HEDGE — AND IT IS TWO CONJUNCTS: A COUNTER OF `0` AT THE CROSSING'S OWN GOVERNED SITES AND THE CROSSED MEMBER OR RECORD STATE INTACT — the admitted tree, the record's `op`/`watched`/`triggered`, the crossed tier set or the constructed instance BYTE-IDENTICAL TO THE UNARMED CONTROL'S.** A `0` with a changed tree or an emptied record is a FAILED cell; a byte-identical tree with a non-zero governed counter is a FAILED cell. **The narrowed counter states WHERE the crossing reads; the crossed datum states WHAT crossed; neither conjunct may stand for the other, and no cell of this unit may quote the `0` alone as its observable.**

**(2f) WHICH CELLS THIS REACHES, AND WHICH IT DOES NOT.** It reaches the four counter-bearing cells re-derived at item `(7)`: `JC-TC-63` (its row `(g)`), `JC-TC-64` (states `(i)`/`(ii)`), `JC-TC-65` (states `(ii)`/`(iii)`) and `JC-TC-66`. **It does NOT reach `JC-TC-62` or `JC-TC-67`, which assert NO counter — a class spelling, a subject and a wall-clock ceiling only — and it does not move `JC-TC-59`'s or `JC-TC-61`'s counters, whose sites are governed ones already (`spellConstraintAddress`/`boundaryReject`, `hasLoneSurrogate`) and which are read through this clause and move in no byte.**

**(3) WHAT THE NARROWING IS NOT.**

**(3a) IT DOES NOT WEAKEN THE LAW.** Item `(1b)`'s operative sentence still forbids a method, iterator, iteration protocol or coercion taken from a mutable prototype ANYWHERE on the six paths, and item `(1c)`'s six lawful forms are unmoved. **The narrowing is a statement about the INSTRUMENT and not about the law: the law's subject is the six paths, and the counter's subject is now the same six paths' sites.**

## §3 — THE OBJECT ARM: `p`, `acc`, `proxy` ARE COPY-IN WALK DECISIONS

**`p`, `acc`, and `proxy` are not members of the value tree.** They are decisions the package's copy-in walk takes while classifying an object: `p` is the prototype classification (`plain`, `foreign`, `null`, or `class`), `acc` is the own-accessor presence decision, and `proxy` is the Proxy decision. They are consumed by the walk; they do not appear in the emitted tree, are not passed to the native half, and are not emitted by the copy-out writer. `[A1 bindings/js/index.mjs:1016 | encodeValue's object arm; bindings/js/src/codec.rs:857 | to_js_value's object arm]`

**Copy-in only.** The native reader has no `p`, `acc`, or `proxy` fields and does not require them. Their absence from a bare JSON object is not a failure. A tagged object that still carries them is an obsolete form and is not produced by this contract's package. `[T]`

**Copy-out indifferent.** The writer emits a plain JSON object from the store's map. It does not emit `p`, `acc`, or `proxy`, and it does not re-run the class decision — the store's own value model cannot produce an outside class on this route. `[A2 docs/decisions.md · A148 Q4 | the retired envelope; A1 src/value.rs:16 | the store's admitted model]`

## §4 — COPY DISCIPLINE: DEEP, ONCE, BOTH DIRECTIONS

**Deep copy in both directions.** Every admitted value crosses as a newly allocated tree on the recipient side. No object, array, or member is shared across the boundary; no member is aliased into two positions; no copy is skipped at any depth. A nested array inside an object is copied as part of the tree, not referenced. `[T]`

**Read once.** Each member and each array element is read exactly once by the walk. A getter cannot occur on an admitted value (an own accessor refuses the whole value, `§2` row 15), so "read once" is a structural property of the admitted tree rather than a promise about a caller's getter. Mutation of a caller's object **after** the walk visited that member is not observed in the crossing; mutation **before** the visit is observed. The crossing takes no snapshot and performs no second deep copy. `[A2 docs/decisions.md · A140 | walk/clone discipline; A1 bindings/js/index.mjs:1016 | the single walk]`

**The copy-out is deep at both sites.** `to_js_value` constructs the JS tree from the store's value; `decodeValue` returns a fresh tree to the caller. Neither site shares a mutable object with the store or with another crossing. `[A2 docs/decisions.md · A148 Q5 | both sites; A1 bindings/js/src/codec.rs:857; bindings/js/index.mjs:1801]`

**The crate's ceilings govern: depth `128`, nodes `1,000,000`.** The package's former depth `64` is re-derived to the crate's `128`; `65` and `66` are admitted depth values, not refusals, and `129` and beyond are refused by row 8's class. `JAS-07` and the greens set's depth cells are re-derived in the owning passes (`§6` row 5). **The node ceiling is the crate's `1,000,000`; exceeding it uses the SAME row 8 class (`a value over the walk ceiling`) and the same status.** `[A2 docs/decisions.md · A148 Q6 | the ceilings; A1 bindings/js/index.mjs:993 | ENCODER_CEILING as the pre-amendment value]`

**The clauses above stand byte-for-byte and are read THROUGH this one; the operative depth `126`, the `127`-and-beyond refusal at the walk before any store verb, the node ceiling's `1,000,000`, the class `a value over the walk ceiling`, the status `ValueRefusedAtBoundary` and the two ceilings themselves are unmoved, and the crate's `128`/`1,000,000` remain the CONVERSION's own bounds.**
**(1) THE NODE INTEGER IS THE VALUE'S OWN NODE COUNT, THE ROOT EXCLUDED.** `§8` row `8`'s integer and `parameter-semantics.md §1` row `8`'s integer is the number of nodes the value carries BELOW its root — its members' values and its elements' values, transitively; **the root is NOT one of them.** The walk's counter is the other number: `bindings/js/index.mjs`'s `encodeValue` adds `1` on entry to every value it visits, the root included (`:1097`), and refuses when that count exceeds `NODE_CEILING` (`1,000,000`, `:1098`). **SO a value of `n` own nodes counts `n+1` and is refused exactly when `n+1 > 1,000,000`, i.e. at `n ≥ 1,000,000`; the admitted own-node range is `0…999,999` and the admitted COUNTED range is `1…1,000,000`.**
**(2) THE ROOT-INCLUSIVE FIGURE IS THE WALK'S COUNTED NUMBER AND NEVER THE DECLARED INTEGER.** A dense root array of exactly `999,999` elements is `999,999` OWN nodes and counts `1,000,000` — **ADMITTED** (measured by the live battery, `LIVE-X2`; driven by `JC-B56`/`LIVE-B56`); a dense root array of exactly `1,000,000` elements is `1,000,000` OWN nodes and counts `1,000,001` — **REFUSED** as row `8`'s class at the ROOT position. **A cell that reads the declared integer as a TOTAL node count would refuse the first of these two, and the build admits it: the row's integer is the own-node count from this date, and every printed `1,000,000`-family figure is the counted number unless it is written as an own-node count.**
**(3) THE TWO CEILINGS' ORDER, AND THE POSITION IT PRODUCES — DECLARED SO A GENERATOR CAN PIN IT.** The walk is PRE-ORDER and single-pass: on ENTRY to each visited value it first adds `1` to the node counter and tests the NODE ceiling (`:1097`–`:1100`), and only then tests the DEPTH ceiling (`:1135`); it records the FIRST fault and stops (`:1083`). **Therefore: (a) a value over ONE ceiling answers that ceiling's arm; (b) a value over BOTH answers the arm whose test first FAILS at the EARLIEST visit in the traversal, and where both would fail at the SAME visit the NODE arm answers, because its test runs first; (c) THE POSITIONS DIFFER AND ARE THE POINT OF THIS DECLARATION — the NODE arm records `path = []` (`:1099`), so its position is the ROOT, `the write's value argument`, and its `<subject>` is the class spelling, while the DEPTH arm records the refused value's own `path` (`:1136`), so its position is that value's `member <path> of the write's value argument` and its `<subject>` is that value's own spelling; (d) a value over both ceilings can therefore answer the ROOT refusal even though its `1,000,001`st counted node lies DEEP, and answers the DEEP refusal only where a `127`-deep path is reached EARLIER in the traversal than the node counter's overflow — the traversal order being array indices ascending (`:1184`) and object keys in `Object.keys` order (`:1200`); (e) the CLASS and the STATUS are shared by both arms, so neither can distinguish them, and only the position and the subject can.**
**(4) THE COPY-OUT CARRIES NO CEILING, AND THAT IS DECLARED HERE RATHER THAN LEFT TO AN UNDECLARED `both`: `bindings/js/src/codec.rs`'s `to_js_value` (`:775`) and `bindings/js/index.mjs`'s `decodeValue` (`:1916`) impose NO depth bound and NO node bound, and no cell drives one, so a stored value beyond either bound crosses OUT unrefused. It is reachable only from OUTSIDE this route's walk, because the commit path cannot store such a value — the walk refuses it before any store verb. `parameter-semantics.md §1`'s rows `7`/`8` therefore read `copy-in` from this date and `§8`'s clause of this date records the register's side of it.** `[A1 bindings/js/index.mjs:1080 · :1097 · :1098 · :1099 · :1135 · :1136 · :1184 · :1200 · :1055 · :1056 · :1916; bindings/js/src/codec.rs:775 | the walk's two counters, their order, the positions they record and the copy-out's absent bound, each opened at this pass's read | numeric or census claim; A2 docs/defects.md · `DEF-2026-10-24-B` · `F1` · `F5` · `F8` | the findings this clause discharges; A2 docs/specs/storeroom-json-crossing-live-report.md §6 · §11.4 | `LIVE-X2`'s measured fixtures | cross-reference]`

**⟶ AMENDED 2026-10-24 — THE CROSSING-DEFECT RULING PASS (`docs/defects.md` · `DEF-2026-10-24-D`, its second finding; `RCA-8(g)`): THE NODE CEILING'S POSITION IS THE ROOT, DECLARED AS THE LAW AND NOT AS A DEFECT — AND THIS DECLARATION LANDS NO CODE CHANGE.**
**The clauses above stand byte-for-byte and are read THROUGH this one; item `(3)` of the 2026-10-24 clause above is re-stated here on ONE term only — the position the NODE arm answers — and its order declarations, its counting convention, the ceilings, the class, the status and the two positions' contrast are unmoved.**
**(1) THE LAW: A VALUE REFUSED AT THE NODE CEILING ANSWERS THE ROOT POSITION — `the write's value argument` — AND NEVER A MEMBER POSITION, HOWEVER DEEP THE NODE AT WHICH THE COUNTER OVERFLOWS.** **The node ceiling is a property of the WHOLE written value and of no member of it:** the walk's counter is global across the traversal (`fault.nodes += 1` at `:1097`, tested against `NODE_CEILING` at `:1098`), so the visit at which it overflows names the node that took the count past `1,000,000` and NOT a member whose own bound was crossed. **The failing TARGET is therefore the written value itself — the value the caller put at the `value` argument — and that value has exactly one position, the root's.**
**(2) WHY THIS IS TRUTHFUL AND NOT MERELY AS-BUILT, WHICH IS THE ONLY GROUND THIS DECLARATION IS ENTITLED TO STAND ON: the DEPTH ceiling IS a property of one path** — the walk tests it on entry to each visited value and records THAT value's own `path` (`:1135`–`:1136`) — **so its position is a member position and `§2.1`'s depth-arm cell prints one. The node ceiling has no such path, and a member position invented for it would name a member that is over no ceiling while the whole value is over one: that would be a FALSE position, and a false position is what this clause is written to forbid, not to mint.**
**(3) THE BUILD ANSWERS EXACTLY THIS TODAY, AND THE CITE NAMES THE SYMBOL AS THE IDENTIFICATION OF RECORD WITH ITS MEASURED LINE BESIDE IT:** the node arm's `refusedValue` call at `:1099` passes `[]` as its `path` argument, and `valuePosition([])` returns `VALUE_ARGUMENT_POSITION` (`:889`, tested at `:891`–`:892`). **SO NO CODE EDIT IS OWED BY THIS ITEM: the arm already answers the position this clause declares, and what was missing was the law and not the byte.**
**(4) WHAT A CELL MAY AND MAY NOT ASSERT HERE: MAY — the node arm's position is `the write's value argument`, its class is the node-ceiling class `a value over the walk ceiling`, its `<subject>` is that same class spelling (`§2.1`'s NODE-arm cell, unmoved) and its status is `ValueRefusedAtBoundary`. MAY NOT — a member position, a member path, or a depth-arm position at this arm; and no cell may require the node arm and the depth arm to agree on a position, because they do not and must not.**
**(5) THE ALTERNATIVE IS NAMED AND IS NOT TAKEN, PLAINLY: if a later pass rules that the node arm must answer a TRUTHFUL DEEP POSITION, THAT IS A CODE CHANGE** — the node arm's fourth argument at `:1099` would have to carry the refused visit's own `path`, and `§2.1`'s node-arm cell and item `(1)` above would then be re-derived with it — **and it is NOT this clause, this pass, or a reading of any clause above it.** **This clause therefore declares the ROOT and leaves the deep position to a ruling that buys it with a byte.**
**(6) `DEF-2026-10-24-D`'s SECOND FINDING IS DISCHARGED AS DECLARED-AND-NOT-A-DEFECT, WITH ITS REASON: the finding reads *"the node arm records `path = []`, so a value refused at the node ceiling answers the ROOT position even when the failing node is deep"* and is correct as a READING of the build; it is refused as a DEFECT because the root is the only position that belongs to a ceiling on the whole value (item `(2)`), and the record's own alternative — a truthful position — is answered at item `(5)` as a code change that no measurement has been filed to demand.** `[A1 bindings/js/index.mjs:889 · :891 · :892 · :1097 · :1098 · :1099 · :1135 · :1136 — the node counter, its test, the node arm's `path` argument, the depth arm's recorded `path` and the position mapping, each opened at this pass's read; A2 docs/defects.md · `DEF-2026-10-24-D` | the finding this clause answers; A2 docs/specs/storeroom-json-crossing/contract.md §2.1 — its NODE-arm cell and its clause of this date | the subject and the position cell this clause reads with | state or fail-state declaration]`

**⟶ AMENDED 2026-10-24 — THE GATE-11 RE-AUDIT PASS (read-only, `2026-10-24`; finding `F-RA-4`; `RCA-8(g)`): THE NODE INTEGER COUNTS THE POSITIONS THE WALK VISITS — A SHARED SUB-VALUE IS COUNTED ONCE PER POSITION — SO THE CEILING BOUNDS THE CROSSING'S OWN WORK AND NOT THE VALUE'S GRAPH.**
**The clauses above stand byte-for-byte and are read THROUGH this one; the operative depth `126`, the node ceiling's `1,000,000`, the class, the status, the two ceilings' order and the node arm's ROOT position are unmoved. THIS clause decides ONE term the `DEF-2026-10-24-B` clause of this date above leaves open, and nothing else.**
**(1) THE UNDEFINED TERM, NAMED AT THE SENTENCE THAT LEAVES IT OPEN: that clause's item `(1)` declares the integer as *"the number of nodes the value carries BELOW its root"* and does not say whether a sub-value reachable from TWO positions is counted ONCE PER OBJECT or ONCE PER POSITION.** A caller's value may alias — `const a = {}; ({x: a, y: a})` reaches one object from two members — and nothing in the walk refuses that. **The term is decided here.**
**(2) THE LAW: THE INTEGER COUNTS VISITS — EACH POSITION THE WALK VISITS BELOW THE ROOT, a sub-value reached from two positions counting TWICE.** `encodeValue` adds `1` on entry to every value it visits (`fault.nodes += 1`, `bindings/js/index.mjs:1328`), tested against `NODE_CEILING` (`1,000,000`, `:1268`) at `:1329`; it carries an ANCESTOR STACK and never a visited set (`ancestors.includes(value)` `:1396`, `next = [...ancestors, value]` `:1401`), which is what ADMITS an acyclic diamond; and it descends once per array element (`:1428`) and once per object member (`:1464`). **SO THE INTEGER IS THE EMITTED TREE'S OWN-NODE COUNT, AND THAT IS THE CONVENTION'S GROUND RATHER THAN A CONVENIENCE: `§4`'s first clause requires the crossed tree to carry no aliasing — every position becomes its OWN node — so a visit count IS a node count of what the crossing builds. The walk's own declared spelling of the figure is the same convention: *"visited-node count"* (`docs/specs/storeroom-js/crossing.md §1.4` item `5`, `:478`).**
**(3) THE TWO STATES THAT DECIDE IT, PRINTED AS ARITHMETIC (measured by the gate-11 re-audit that filed `F-RA-4`, and NOT by a cell of this unit's red set): (a) A DIAMOND — `const a = {}; ({x: a, y: a})` — carries `2` distinct objects and counts `3` visits (the root `1`, then `1` at each member) and is ADMITTED. (b) A ROOT ARRAY OF `1,000,000` ELEMENTS ALL THE SAME LEAF — `new Array(1_000_000).fill(leaf)` — carries `2` distinct nodes and counts `1,000,001` visits (the root `1`, then `1` per element) and is REFUSED as row `8`'s class `a value over the walk ceiling` at the ROOT position with the status `ValueRefusedAtBoundary`.** **THEIR DISTINCT-NODE COUNTS ARE THE SAME ORDER AND THEIR ANSWERS DIFFER, WHICH IS THE POINT: the integer is not a census of the caller's graph.**
**(4) WHY THIS IS THE TRUTHFUL BOUND AND NOT MERELY THE AS-BUILT ONE: the ceiling's subject is the CROSSING'S OWN WORK — the visits the walk performs and the nodes the copy allocates — and a distinct-node census of the source bounds neither, because a `2`-distinct-node value can cost `1,000,000` copies.** A ceiling that bounded the graph would admit the filled array and then perform the very work it exists to bound.
**(5) WHAT A CELL MAY AND MAY NOT DERIVE, SO NO PROPERTY IS WRITTEN FROM A DISTINCT-NODE CENSUS: MAY — count visits (equivalently the emitted tree's own nodes below the root) and assert item `(3)`'s two states, the boundary `999,999` admitted / `1,000,000` refused of that clause's item `(2)`, and `§8` row `8`'s integer. MAY NOT — refuse an acyclic diamond for its repeated sub-value (`JC-TC-11`'s admission stands); admit a value because its distinct-node count is small; derive the node integer from a `Set` of objects, from `structuredClone`'s answer, or from any census that DE-DUPLICATES positions; or assert that two values with equal distinct-node counts answer alike.** **The two states of item `(3)` are carried inside `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-42` and `JC-TC-11` cells at that part's `§3` clause of this date, and no cell claims to have run them from this clause.**
**(6) NOTHING ELSE MOVES: the depth arm's position and subject, the node arm's ROOT position (that clause's items `(1)`–`(5)`), the class, the status, the `1,000,000` figure and `§8` row `8`'s range `0…999,999` stand; the register's row `8` MEANING cell is re-derived in place at `§8`'s clause of this date, that cell being where a property is written from.** `[A1 bindings/js/index.mjs:1268 · :1328 · :1329 · :1396 · :1401 · :1428 · :1464 — the node counter, its ceiling, its test, the ancestor stack and the two descents, each opened at this pass's read | numeric or census claim; A2 docs/specs/storeroom-js/crossing.md §1.4 item `5` (`:478`) | the walk's own *"visited-node count"* spelling | cross-reference]`

**⟶ AMENDED 2026-10-24 — THE GATE-11 RE-AUDIT PASS (read-only, `2026-10-24`; finding `F-RA-9`; `RCA-8(g)`): ITEM `(4)` OF THE `DEF-2026-10-24-B` CLAUSE OF THIS DATE ABOVE IS MARKED A **CODE-READING CLAIM** — THE COPY-OUT CARRIES NO CEILING AND NO REACHABLE OVER-MAXIMUM WITNESS EXISTS ON THAT ROUTE — SO NO CELL IS EXPECTED AND NONE IS OWED.**
**The `DEF-2026-10-24-B` clause of this date above and its item `(4)` stand byte-for-byte and are read THROUGH this one; the copy-out's unboundedness, the two sites, the direction narrowing at `parameter-semantics.md §1`, the register's note at `§8` and every ceiling figure are unmoved. THIS clause adds the claim's KIND and its REASON, and nothing else.**
**(1) THE CLAIM AND ITS KIND IN ONE LINE: *"a stored value beyond either bound crosses OUT unrefused"* is a CODE-READING CLAIM** — taken from `bindings/js/src/codec.rs`'s `to_js_value` (`:775`) and `bindings/js/index.mjs`'s `decodeValue` (`:2159`), **neither of which counts a depth or a node** — **and it is NOT a measured state of any witness.**

**`-0` does not cross as a sign.** The crossing normalizes `-0` to `0`; the store's own sign behavior is not a crossing promise (`A140`'s pin stands). `[A2 docs/decisions.md · A140]`

## §5 — THE COPY-OUT FORM: THE SAME BARE TREE AT BOTH SITES

**Both copy-out sites carry the same bare tree.** The native half's `to_js_value` and the JavaScript half's `decodeValue` return strings, booleans, finite numbers, dense arrays, and plain objects. Neither emits `{k:'s'|'b'|'n'|'g'|'F'|'z'|'u'|'a'|'o'}`, a class token, or a `{"v":…}` envelope. `[A2 docs/decisions.md · A148 Q4 · Q5; A1 bindings/js/src/codec.rs:857; bindings/js/index.mjs:1801]`

**The outside arms are unproduced, not deleted by this contract.** The Rust `JsValue` outside variants remain declared for the Rust surface and are not removed by this unit; the copy-out writer's arms for them remain declared but unproduced on this route. The package's `decodeValue` has no outside-class arm because the store's admitted value cannot yield one. `[A1 bindings/js/src/crossing.rs:130 | JsValue; bindings/js/src/codec.rs:857 | the writer's arms]`

**Retired forms.** The `{"v":…}` envelope and the `{"e":…}` side-channel are dead for this crossing. The tagged form survives only where `§6` names a residue and an owning unit's pass has not yet moved it. `[A2 docs/decisions.md · A140 · A148 Q4]`

**⟶ AMENDED 2026-10-24 — THE GATE-11 THIRD AUDIT PASS (read-only, `2026-10-24`; finding `F-RA-15`; `RCA-8(g)`): THE COPY-OUT'S OBJECT PROTOTYPE IS **NOT PINNED** — THE CONTRACT PINS THE TREE AND NOT THE ALLOCATION — SO THE CELL THAT ASSERTED THE TEST REALM'S `Object.prototype` IS NARROWED TO THE ADMITTED COPY-OUT OBJECT SHAPES, AND THAT CELL EDIT IS OWED TO THE TEST WRITER; NO CODE CHANGE IS OWED.**
**The two copy-out paragraphs, the retired-forms paragraph and `§3`'s copy-out indifference stand byte-for-byte and are read THROUGH this one; the bare tree, the no-tag rule, the retired envelope, the outside variants left unproduced and the copy-out's absent ceiling are unmoved.**
**(1) WHAT THE PARAGRAPH ABOVE PINS, AND WHAT IT DOES NOT: *"strings, booleans, finite numbers, dense arrays, and plain objects"* pins the TREE'S DATA SHAPE — the form that carries no `{k:…}` tag, no class token and no `{"v":…}` envelope — and it pins NO PROTOTYPE, because a prototype is not a member of the tree and THE STORE'S OWN VALUE MODEL RECORDS NO PROTOTYPE AT ALL** (`src/value.rs`'s admitted taxonomy, `§1`'s authority). **`§1` admits TWO object forms and the copy-out must be able to answer either: the PLAIN limb (`§1`'s `(1)`, a prototype-carrying, terminal, standard-shaped prototype) and the `null`-PROTOTYPE OBJECT (`§1`'s `(1)(a)` and `§3`'s `p` classification `null`).**
**(2) THE RULING, AND WHICH ROUTE WAS TAKEN: THE CELL IS NARROWED; THE CONTRACT IS NOT WIDENED TO PIN THE PROTOTYPE.** **The three grounds, each decisive on its own: (a) pinning *"the caller realm's `Object.prototype`"* would be FALSE for a caller in a `vm` realm driving the module loaded in another realm — `decodeValue` (`:2277`) mints its objects in the MODULE'S realm and `to_js_value` (`bindings/js/src/codec.rs:775`) through the addon's own — so the pin would need a realm clause no clause of this contract states and no measurement has taken; (b) a lawful copy-out that returned a `null`-prototype tree — a form `§1` admits and `§3` names — FAILS THE CELL TODAY, which is exactly the direction this finding reports, the cell asserting more than the spec pins; and (c) THIS SECTION'S SUBJECT IS THE ABSENCE OF A TAG, A CLASS TOKEN OR AN ENVELOPE, and a prototype is none of those three.**
**(3) THE NARROWED ASSERTION, EXACTLY, SO ONE FORM GOVERNS EVERY COPY-OUT OBJECT CELL: a copy-out object (a) carries exactly the stored OWN ENUMERABLE STRING-KEYED member set, with no tag, class token or envelope in any member; (b) is NOT an array, NOT a `Proxy`, and NOT any other prototype-carrying non-plain object — equivalently it satisfies `§1`'s object limb OR is a `null`-prototype object (`§3`'s `p = 'null'`); and (c) is a FRESH tree sharing no mutable member with the store or with another crossing (`§4`'s copy discipline, unmoved).** **AND NO CELL MAY ASSERT A PROTOTYPE IDENTITY ON THE COPY-OUT — neither `Object.getPrototypeOf(back) === Object.prototype` NOR its negation at a named realm.**
**(4) THE CONSEQUENCE, NAMED PER CELL AND OWNED: `bindings/js/test/negative-generators.test.mjs`'s `NG-JS-Q5` asserts `Object.getPrototypeOf(back) !== Object.prototype` at `:4325` — a prototype identity this contract does NOT pin — and THAT CELL MUST MOVE to item `(3)`'s form; the edit is the TEST WRITER'S and NO CODE CHANGE is owed.** **AND `test-cases.md`'s `JC-TC-14` (*"a fresh plain object"*) and `JC-TC-15` (*"the same tree, deeply copied"*) are READ THROUGH item `(3)` at that part's clause of this date, with NO ID MOVING.** **This is the only one of this pass's six findings whose repair is a CELL and neither a byte nor a clause of the build's behaviour, and it is recorded here rather than filed as a defect so that the unit's own copy-out claim is never stronger than its law.**
**(5) THE FINDING'S SECOND HALF IS DECLARED AT ITS OWN HOME AND NOT HERE, AND THE POINTER IS THE WHOLE OF THIS ITEM: `parameter-semantics.md §2`'s clause of this date declares that NO ESCAPING IS APPLIED TO A POSITION SEGMENT — which is what makes `NG-JS-Q4`'s asserted unescaped spellings lawful and moves no cell — so no reader takes the two halves of `F-RA-15` as one defect.**
**(6) WHAT DOES NOT MOVE: the bare tree at both copy-out sites, the `__proto__`-member form (gate `4`'s `G4-2`: own data properties defined on copy-out, `Object.fromEntries` in `decodeValue`), the copy-out's absent ceiling (`§4`'s clause of `2026-10-24` on `F-RA-9`), the outside variants kept declared and unproduced, and `§6`'s residue rows and owners.**

- No outside value reaches the store on the JavaScript route (`§2`).
